### 4. Kairouz
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2026-07-08 · визит: 2026-07-03 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Egypt

Плюсы: We had a wonderful stay at JAZ Elite Casa Del Mar and enjoyed every moment. From the first day until check-out, everything was excellent. The hotel is very clean, the rooms are comfortable, and the beach and pools are beautiful, clean, and perfect for relaxing.

The food was one of the best parts of our stay. Everything was fresh, very tasty, and there was a great variety every day. Chef Sabri and Executive Chef Mohamed were always checking if everything was okay and if we needed anything, which we really appreciated.

A special thank you to Nancy at the Front Office for the warm welcome and all her help. Thank you also to Mahmoud Thaibt, Mahmoud Shawky, and Ammar from Palmira Restaurant. They were always kind, friendly, and gave us great service. Mina at the pool and Ashraf at the Splash Pool were also amazing and always made sure we had everything we needed.

We also want to thank Mrs. Vanessa and Mr. Joseph from the management team for taking the time to ask about our stay and making sure everything was perfect. The animation team was fantastic and made every day fun with different activities.

A big thank you as well to the housekeeping team for keeping our room clean every day and for always being friendly.

Everyone made us feel welcome and at home. This was one of our best holidays, and we can't wait to come back. We highly recommend JAZ Elite Casa Del Mar to anyone visiting Hurghada. Thank you to the whole team for making our vacation so special.

### 7. Yasmina
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2026-07-01 · визит: 2026-06-20 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: France

**Parfait**

Parfait

Плюсы: Nous avons passé un séjour absolument merveilleux au Jazz Elite Casa Del Mar Beach. Tout était parfait du début à la fin : les chambres étaient impeccables, la nourriture variée et délicieuse, les installations très bien entretenues et l’ambiance agréable et reposante.

Ce qui a rendu notre séjour encore plus spécial, c’est la gentillesse, le professionnalisme et le sourire de tout le personnel. Un immense merci à toute l’équipe pour son accueil, sa disponibilité et tous les efforts fournis pour que nous nous sentions comme chez nous.

Nous souhaitons adresser un remerciement tout particulier à Alaa, dont la bienveillance, la gentillesse et l’attention ont véritablement marqué notre séjour. Sa bonne humeur et son dévouement font toute la différence. C’est grâce à des personnes comme lui que l’on repart avec de si beaux souvenirs.

Merci infiniment à toute l’équipe du Jazz Elite Casa Del Mar Beach pour ces magnifiques vacances. Nous reviendrons avec grand plaisir et recommanderons cet hôtel à tous nos proches !

Минусы: Tout était parfait

### 12. Hanaa
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2026-06-13 · визит: 2026-06-07 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Morocco

**Loved It as usual! :)**

Loved It as usual! :)

Плюсы: This was my fourth stay with the Jaz group and my second stay at Jaz Casa del Mar Beach, and once again I enjoyed many aspects of the hotel. The food was good, the location is excellent, the beach is beautiful, and the rooms are comfortable and well maintained. Cleanliness standards were consistently high throughout my stay. Most importantly, many staff members were friendly, welcoming, and genuinely willing to help, which contributed to a pleasant holiday. experience. Overall, the hotel continues to offer a relaxing atmosphere and the quality service that has made me a loyal guest of the Jaz brand over the years.

Минусы: Having stayed at Jaz Casa del Mar Beach in 2023 and again in 2026, while also visiting several other Jaz properties in between, I was expecting to see a clear added value from the current « Elite » Concept. Unfortunately ,the prices has increased dramatically yet I did not experience a corresponding improvement in facilities, services, or overall guest experience. As a loyal Jaz guest, I feel that the Elite branding currently does not deliver enough tangible benefits to justify the significant increase in price!

### 20. Leila
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2026-05-10 · визит: 2026-05-02 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Austria

**Perfect holiday**

Perfect holiday

Плюсы: I’ve stayed in so many hotels in Hurghada, but this is the only one I’ve visited twice and for good reason!! Everything here is simply perfect: the service, the food, the cleanliness, and especially the people.
The absolute highlight of this hotel is the food. I have never eaten this well anywhere else in Hurghada, and I’ve truly stayed at many different hotels. You can enjoy every meal without worrying about getting sick or not tolerating the food, which can sometimes be a concern in Egypt. Here, dining is a real pleasure. Even the head chef personally comes around to ask if everything is okay and makes sure every guest is satisfied.
The hotel offers many different areas, so there’s something for everyone. If you want peace and relaxation, there’s a quiet pool area, while the main pool is always full of fun and great energy.
Shams is an amazing person. If I had to describe someone with the perfect Egyptian charm, it would definitely be him. He and his animation team are incredibly kind, friendly, and always make sure guests are having a great time. At the same time, they are very respectful! If you want to relax and be left alone, they completely respect that as well. I already loved the entertainment team during my first stay, but now they’ve become even better. And Mohamed from the beach bar was always attentive and made sure we were well taken care of at all times.
In my opinion, this is definitely the best hotel in Hurghada! Both in terms of quality and the outstanding staff.

---

Additionally, many thanks to Ms. Larisa for her kindness, attentiveness, and dedication—she truly makes a difference in the overall guest experience.
It is rare to find a hotel that maintains such high standards over time, and this is the reason I keep coming back. I wholeheartedly recommend this hotel to anyone looking for a reliable, welcoming, and high-quality stay.
Looking forward to my next visit!

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2026-03-02 · визит: 2026-02-26 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Austria

Плюсы: Die Anlage ist sehr schön und weitläufig und es gibt mehrere Pools (teils beheizt). Die Mitarbeiter sind alle sehr freundlich und sehr bemüht. Der Strand ist sehr sauber und schön. Am Buffet gibt es sehr viel Auswahl. Ca 10 min Fahrt zum Flughafen mit dem Taxi.

Минусы: Unser Zimmer hatte leider eine Verbindungstüre zum Nebenzimmer mitten im Raum, dadurch war es sehr hellhörig und man konnte alle Gespräche und WC Gänge vom Nebenzimmer mithören. Wir wollten das Zimmer daher bereits am 1. Tag wechseln doch leider konnte die Rezeption uns kein anderes Zimmer anbieten. Das Management Team hat sich am Ende aber auch nochmal mit einem Obstkorb bei uns entschuldigt. Leider wird direkt neben dem Hotel gebaut und es war daher von der Früh weg Baustellenlärm. An den Pools und am Strand gibt es den ganzen Tag Animation, es ist dementsprechend leider auch hier ziemlich laut - es wäre schön, wenn es hier auch einen Bereich geben würde in dem man mehr Ruhe hat.

### 44. Steve
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2026-02-02 · визит: 2026-01-28 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: United Kingdom

**Paradise by the Red Sea.**

Paradise by the Red Sea.

Плюсы: My wife and I stayed here for 5 nights leaving the kids at home with the grandparents. We came wanting to chill and do nothing and we pretty much got that. The hotel set up is amazing, you can have a drink while chilling in the pool and looking at the Red Sea.
The cocktail options were amazing. I loved the lunch time restaurant next to the pool. Private beach and ocean were spotless and you could book tours and spa treatments while relaxing on a sunbed. There were always sunbeds available and the lifeguard would take peoples towels away if they left for more than an hour which is a great idea. The room was clean and comfortable. The Spa was well priced for a 5 star hotel and very good, all Thai ladies. The entertainment crew had high energy, always came to say hello and tried to include everyone in the hotel. I have so many staff to thank but can’t remember all the names.

Минусы: The evening entertainment could be enhanced to reflect the clientele. More professional. The karaoke was not our vibe but the next night was amazing with the performances and singers but would be nice to see some consistency.

---

**紅海のリゾート滞在を満喫できるホテル**

紅海のリゾート滞在を満喫できるホテル

Плюсы: 美しい紅海に面したホテルはリゾート感にあふれていて、部屋は広く、ベッドルームが２つあり、娘と私でゆっくり眠ることができました。スタッフも大変親切で、こちらの個人的なトラブルに対しても親身に相談に乗ってくれ、無事に解決することができました。レセプションのBadyさんには心よりお礼申し上げます。車の手配をしてくれたTaxi ServiceのYasserさんも本当によい方でした。飛行機の関係で朝5時にチェックアウトしたのですが、お弁当が用意され、フロントのMohamedさんが笑顔で送ってくれました。ホテル内で申し込んだ砂漠と海での乗馬も楽しかったです。

Минусы: 朝や夜にシャワーを使う時、お湯が出てくるのにずいぶん時間がかかりました。

### 71. Carl
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-11-03 · визит: 2025-10-25 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: United Kingdom

**We will be going back same time next year if the price is similar.**

We will be going back same time next year if the price is similar.

Плюсы: The hotel and grounds is on the small side compared to some we have stayed at during our 15 visits the Egypt, but when staying for a week it’s ideal as it only takes you a few hours to figure out where everything is and what’s on offer.
The hotel is immaculate with fantastic staff. Nearly everyone goes out of there way to be helpful and make your stay enjoyable.
Buffet is small but the presentation and standard of food is good. We took breakfast there each day with lunch split between there amd the Aqua daytime snack bar.
Evening meals were taken in one of the three A La Cartes. We ventured out of the hotel on two evening to the Taj Indian restaurant (5-10 minutes walk) and in the New Harbour (20 minutes in a taxi @10€ each way)
Definitely worth a stay and we will be returning next year.
Special mention to Kameel the fantastic poolside waiter who delivered endless drinks to our sunbeds every day.
Hussein (and his brother who he blames for everything 😆) at the Oscar, Aqua and Pomodoro restaurants. He is tireless and so full of fun.
Lastly to Gamal Mostafa the restaurants manager who seemed to be everywhere and who made our stay amazing with his efficiency.
I could go on mentioning others, but will stop there.

Минусы: Only two things:

- There is only one shower cubicle available near the reception pool to shower with nowhere private to dry and dress.

Der Aufenthalt war sehr gut. Der Hinflug (Business mit Condor ab München gebucht) war sehr schlecht!

Ein idealer Ort, wenn man wirklich entspannen möchte.

Плюсы: Das Hotel war sehr ruhig und nicht überfüllt, auch gegen Mittag haben wir immer einen guten Platz am Strand bekommen und es wird wirklich Wert auf Qualität gelegt statt Quantität. Unser Zimmer war sehr schön, die Betten bequem und die Ausstattung top. Das Animationsteam fand ich besonders toll, sie hatten alle immer gute Laune und haben die Gäste super unterhalten. Besonders positiv aufgefallen ist mir der Dienstleistungsservice; uns wurden keine Touren oder Massagen mehrfach angeboten. Wir wurden freundlich gefragt und bei einem „Nein“ wurden wir in Ruhe gelassen - sie waren auf keinen Fall aufdringlich!

Минусы: Der Abfluss in unserer Dusche war oft verstopft und auch die öffentliche Dusche neben dem Pool für Erwachsene. Das Essen hätte abwechslungsreicher sein können.

---

### 111. Marina
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-09-03 · визит: 2025-08-20 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: France

**Parfait**

Parfait

Плюсы: J’ai tellement apprécié mon séjour que j’ai décidé de le prolonger, c’est dire à quel point je me sens bien dans cet hôtel !
Un grand merci à Monsieur Ibrahim, toujours attentif et attentionné, qui venait régulièrement prendre de nos nouvelles et a rendu notre séjour encore plus agréable. Merci également à Islam, au bar de la réception, pour ses délicieux cocktails et pour s’être toujours occupé de nous avec une grande gentillesse.
Et bien sûr, un immense merci à l’incroyable équipe d’animation – liza, taisha, mimi, ella, alica, shams, marawan, moody, tyga et Ramy– toujours souriants, dynamiques et pleins d’énergie. Grâce à eux, chaque journée est rythmée par de superbes activités, de la bonne humeur et des moments inoubliables.

### 112. Marina

Плюсы: Un séjour inoubliable grâce à l’incroyable équipe d’animation ! Toujours souriante, dynamique et pleine d’énergie, elle a su mettre l’ambiance du matin jusqu’au soir. Les activités sont variées et adaptées à tous : sport, jeux, spectacles… impossible de s’ennuyer. Mention spéciale pour les beach parties et les pool parties, organisées avec beaucoup de créativité et une super atmosphère festive. On sent vraiment que l’équipe aime ce qu’elle fait et ça rend le séjour encore plus agréable. Merci pour tous ces beaux souvenirs, vous êtes au top !

### 123. Elsayed
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-08-27 · визит: 2025-08-25 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Egypt

**Everything is perfect**

Everything is perfect

Плюсы: I had the pleasure of staying at Jaz Elite during my short vacation in Egypt, and honestly, it turned out to be one of the best hotel experiences I’ve ever had. At first, I just came to check the place as I usually like to try different hotels. It looked fine and acceptable to me at the beginning, but once I actually checked in, it became a journey of amazement.

1. Reception & Staff:
From the very first moment, I was impressed by the kindness, professionalism, and helpfulness of the entire team. Everyone was genuinely willing to assist with a smile and incredible courtesy. The comfort and peace of mind I felt here was something I rarely experience, whether in Egypt or abroad.

2. Food Quality:
I’m someone who is not easily satisfied when it comes to food, but here I was amazed. Every single day during my 5-night stay, the quality remained outstanding. The dishes were delicious, diverse, and carefully prepared. The level of quality control was remarkable, and the variety was simply perfect.

3. Cleanliness & Nature:
The cleanliness throughout the property was excellent. The surrounding natural views were both beautiful and relaxing, adding so much charm to the whole stay.

4. Kids’ Club:
The kids’ club was beyond amazing. The staff there were truly talented in dealing with children and creating a fun and joyful environment. My daughter was so happy and entertained the whole time.

Special Thanks:
I want to give special thanks to some incredible people who made our stay unforgettable. Coincidentally, it was my daughter’s birthday, and the team went above and beyond to celebrate it in the sweetest way, making her so happy.
• Mr. Gamal
• Abdullah Hammam
• Ahmed El Behery
• The Restaurant Manager & all the restaurant staff

Final Note:
As a frequent traveler, I can confidently say that this level of hospitality and professionalism is very rare to find.

Минусы: honestly have no negative comments about my stay. The only area that could use a little improvement is the desserts section, as it could be presented at a higher level. However, this did not affect my overall experience at all, which was truly outstanding.

### 145. Mama
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **6.0** · опубликован: 2025-08-19 · визит: 2025-08-11 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Germany

**Ein paar Mängel im Aufenthalt**

Ein paar Mängel im Aufenthalt

Плюсы: Mir hat besonders gut gefallen: die große Auswahl beim Essen, die Sauberkeit des Restaurants und der Schwimmbereich.
Man würde ständig gefragt ob alles okay sei.

Минусы: Das Zimmer entsprach in Bezug auf Möbel und Sauberkeit leider nicht unseren Erwartungen: Teile der Möbel waren kaputt, der Vorhang hing nicht richtig im Scharnier, die Bettdecke war nicht sauber, der Abfluss stark geruchlich belastet und das Bad verstopft. Der Wasserkocher war schmutzig. Der Kleiderschrank war für drei Personen zu klein, sodass eine Person ihre Kleidung im Koffer lassen musste. Obwohl man nachfragt, ob alles in Ordnung ist, werden Probleme kaum gelöst, was den Eindruck erweckt, dass die Fürsorge eher oberflächlich ist. Die Cocktails waren sehr einseitig und boten nur wenig Auswahl. Zudem hatten wir das Gefühl, dass ohne Trinkgeld die Behandlung schlechter ausfällt.

### 147. Lena
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-08-19 · визит: 2025-08-08 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: France

**Très bel hôtel**

Très bel hôtel

Плюсы: La vue exceptionnelle sur la mer !
L’écoute attentive des managers
L’amabilité du personnel qui est très attentionné
La propreté de l’établissement
Les animations le soir
La plage
Les très jolies chambres
Le confort de la literie
Le confort des equipments de salle de bain
Le calme dans les chambres
Pas de queue dans le restaurant
Tout est mis à disposition pour que les clients se sentent bien.
Les ados ont adoré ce séjour où ils se sont fait plein de copains. L’équipe d’animation est au top.

Минусы: Les repas :
- Des buffets un peu répétitifs.
- Les pizzas et les pains de qualité très moyenne.
- Une grosse tourista pour toute la famille le dernier jour…. Peut-être un problème de traitement de l’eau ?

- Les prix très élevés du diving center
- Les prix très élevés des taxis (préférez uber !). 10 fois plus cher si commandés dans l’hôtel.
- Les tarifs de la clinique qui est dans l’hôtel, une folie ! 1390€ pour 1 consultation et des antibiotiques….
L’hôtel n’est pas responsable de la clinique je le précise, mais mieux vaut aller dans une pharmacie tout près de l’hôtel plutôt que d’aller dans cette clinique.

### 156. Hugo
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **6.0** · опубликован: 2025-08-16 · визит: 2025-08-05 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: France

**30 Connecting Rooms -no privacity**

30 Connecting Rooms -no privacity

Плюсы: Positives:
• The food is very good, varied, and of high quality.
• The dessert service is excellent, with plenty of delicious options.
• The themed restaurants offer great menus and enjoyable dining experiences.
• The entertainment team is excellent, always energetic and engaging.
• Hygiene and cleanliness in the common areas are well maintained.
• There are many swimming pools, all with plenty of sunbeds available.
• The reception staff, although not able to accommodate my request for a non-connecting room, still tried to respond positively.

Минусы: Negatives:
• I noticed that no women work in restaurants, housekeeping, or general hotel services (only in reception and animation), which is disappointing in terms of inclusivity and hospitality standards.
• The beach is right next to a docking station for boats, so the water sometimes has oil residue, and the sand is uncomfortable to walk on.
• I was given a connecting room with no privacy or sound insulation. I was told that 30 rooms are like this, which is unacceptable for a hotel of this level.
• The spa is extremely overpriced – even more expensive than in many European cities, including Paris.
• The staff show a lack of proper training in hospitality, and there is some disorganization.

### 170. Ihsane
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2025-08-10 · визит: 2025-08-03 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: France

**Excellent séjour près du centre d’Hurghada**

Excellent séjour près du centre d’Hurghada

Плюсы: Ce que j’ai aimé :
- Les 4 piscines surtout celle face à la plage
- La plage privé
- Le buffet à volonté très bon et très varié, avec des nouveautés presque chaque jour
- Les transat « canapé » pour se relaxer au bord des différentes piscines
- La chambre et la salle de bain spacieuses
- La très jolie rue où est situé l’hôtel
- La salle de sport équipée Technogym
- La piscine réservée aux adultes où vous serez presque toujours seul
- Le Pomodoro, le restaurant italien, c’est un des trois restaurants « À la carte » au sein de l’hôtel. On y mange très bien.
- Les cuisiniers du Aqua Snacks Restaurants, ils sont super gentils et sympa malgré qu’ils travaillent sous la chaleurs ils ont toujours le sourire, merci à Amer pour sa gentillesse

Минусы: Ce que je n’ai pas aimé :
- Le personnel qui n’explique pas très bien le fonctionnement du All Inclusive, ni ce qu’il y a dans l’hôtel (3 restaurants mais non inclus dans le All Inclusive), plusieurs bars avec des fonctions différentes. Nous sommes un peu livré à nous même avec uniquement un QR Code qui ouvre un PDF avec des indications mais l’hôtel est très grand et tout repérer peut être assez compliqué. Et quand on demande un renseignement on a parfois l’impression de déranger…
- Le Wi-Fi avec un débit vraiment très bas
- Il manque des coussins/matelas dans la piscine pour se relaxer, ça aurait été un gros +

### 172. Y
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2025-08-09 · визит: 2025-08-01 · компания: Couple · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Algeria

**Un coup de cœur pour le service et l'ambiance**

Un coup de cœur pour le service et l'ambiance

Плюсы: Notre séjour dans cet hôtel a été très apprécié, et nous recommandons vivement l'établissement pour son service et son ambiance.

Un grand merci à toute l'équipe d'animation qui a été exceptionnelle. Leur professionnalisme et leur bonne humeur ont rendu notre séjour mémorable. Un énorme coup de cœur pour Marwane, dont la gentillesse et le sourire ont su nous toucher et faire de notre voyage une expérience inoubliable.

Concernant les points à améliorer, nous avons été dérangés par le bruit du climatiseur dans la chambre ainsi que par le chantier situé à côté de l'hôtel. Ces éléments ont malheureusement un peu affecté notre tranquillité.

Dans l'ensemble, nous gardons un excellent souvenir de notre voyage grâce à la qualité du service et de l'animation.

### 183. Belinda
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-08-01 · визит: 2025-07-22 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Austria

**Perfekt**

Perfekt

Плюсы: Besser kann es nicht sein. Essen war ein Traum man hat 3 mal die chance a la Card zu essen auf anfrage darf man 2 mal im gleichen restaurant gehn. Alle Restaurants auch Buffe ein Traum. Waren 10 tage dort kein einzieges mal Magenprobleme gehabt. Die Kellner fragen dich den Ganzen tag am Pool am Meer egal wo du bist ob du was zum Trinken willst. Es gibt am Pool Cornetto Eis ganzen tag zur freien Wahl. Hotel und Zimmer und Bad sehr modern und Sauber. Bett sehr gemütlich. Man merkt dass es alles Neu ist. Wer Fisch oder Meeresfrüchte liebt kommt hier umso mehr aus seine Kosten.
Die Lage ist in ganz Hurghada die schönste moderste und reichste ort. Alles voll mit 5 sterne Hotels. Aus dem grund alles sauber und sehr sehr schön.
Ich werde sicher wieder kommen.

Минусы: Alles perfekt

### 201. Pierre-arnaud
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-07-21 · визит: 2025-07-05 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: France

**Magnifique hôtel ! Tout était parfait.**

Magnifique hôtel ! Tout était parfait.

Плюсы: Magnifique hôtel ! Tout était parfait.
Le personnel est adorable, accueillant, très serviable et aux petits soins, que ce soient le personnel d'entretien, aux étages, aux bars ou aux snacks, au restaurant, les animateurs...
La vue depuis l'hôtel, que ce soit du haut de la verrière ou depuis la grande piscine à débordement qui donne sur la plage est à couper le souffle... on ne s'en lasse pas.
Les chambres sont spacieuses et très bien équipées avec une excellente literie et des grands lits.
Le buffet ainsi que les trois restaurants ont et du choix et de la qualité. Il y en a pour tous les goûts, c'est varié et vraiment délicieux. Les plages d'ouvertures sont larges qui fait qu'il y a presque toujours un des lieux (snack..) ouvert pour manger.
Les piscines sont très agréables avec plusieurs espaces séparés les uns des autres, quant à la plage attenante à l'hotel, elle est très agréable aussi.m, très propre.
Il y a de nombreuses animations tout au long de la journée, non intrusives, les animateurs sont très sympas, dynamiques et à l'écoute et ils savent mettre une bonne ambiance décontractée et sympathique.

Минусы: RAS

---

Good food, not so good beach and water.

Плюсы: The food is amazing and Haythem who is responsible for the food was wonderful. The staff was extremely nice and diligent in getting us what we needed with a smile.

Минусы: The water was pretty awful. For the price I would have expected the beach and water to be 100x better. The water wasn't clear at all. ### 214. Kamil
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-07-14 · визит: 2025-07-02 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Poland

Плюсы: Food! OMG most of the food they serve is restaurant quality or close to it 🤤 Similar with drinks - very tasty and quite strong 🤭 And I'd say this is the most important differentiating factor when comparing JAZ to other hotels - if you want to eat / drink very good just don't look further.
Another big positive - there are lots of sunbeds, no need to reserve them early in the morning 👌
All of the other areas linked with our stay where top as well - hotel is spotless, rooms are modern and spacious, entertainment is of high quality attending to different tastes, beach and sea breathtaking, chillful shisha corner, amazing staff (!!!), and many many more small things making our stay epic 😎

### 215. Billard
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-07-12 · визит: 2025-06-28 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: France

**Sublime**

Sublime

Плюсы: Hôtel juste parfait ! La nourriture est bonne même si pas trop variée mais au bout de 15 jours c’est logique, le personnel est juste parfait, mes remerciements vont droit à : Noha (coucou Bar) et Mohamed Ahmed (bar de la piscine) pour avoir rendu notre séjour meilleur par votre gentillesse ! pour la Team Animation : Merci à Moody et Lena, meilleurs animateurs !

Минусы: Pour le côté négatif : pas de brosse à WC dans aucuns toilettes à l’exception de vos chambres, les serveurs du bar des autres piscines sont juste désagréables principalement le serveur : Islam, il dit jamais bonjour, râle toujours quand on lui demande quelque chose, juste pour l’ambiance je préfère la piscine principale. Et les paroles de douche ne sont pas pratiques car trop petite donc salle de bain infondée a la sortie.

Merci pour l’accueil, je recommande !

### 241. Maroua
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-06-20 · визит: 2025-06-16 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Luxembourg

**Highly recommended, espically with newborns**

Highly recommended, espically with newborns

Плюсы: Simply amazing. I’ve been to Hurghada many times and stayed in several high-end resorts, but Jaz Elite is by far the best experience I’ve had.

At first, I was a bit unsure because of its size, but honestly—even at full occupancy—it never felt crowded.

Some highlights: the room was probably the best I’ve ever stayed in (I chose the deluxe for extra space—definitely worth it). The food is excellent at the main restaurant; I recommend booking at Oscar for the à la carte experience. And don’t miss the pharaonic entertainment show—it’s honestly the best I’ve seen anywhere in Egypt.

What really surprised me is that it’s rated 4 stars, but the service and quality easily beat many 5-star resorts in Hurghada. It really feels more like a European standard in many ways, especially when it comes to cleanliness. The whole place is very accessible too, whether you’re using a wheelchair or have a stroller.

Highly recommended.

---

### 247. Hazem
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2025-06-15 · визит: 2025-06-07 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Egypt

Плюсы: Location
Foood was really amazing and best thing in the hotel
Beach
Pool

Минусы: This was our third consecutive summer staying at this hotel, but unfortunately, the experience continues to fall short, especially when it comes to communication and service from the reception team.

We made several requests – a king-size bed and two rooms next to each other since we are two families traveling together. None of these requests were fulfilled. Despite being returning guests, we felt like we were treated as if it was our first time.

We also discovered by chance from the animation team that we were supposed to be wearing green bracelets to be recognized as hotel guests. No one informed us of this during check-in. When we raised this at check-out, the reception admitted it was their mistake.

Another major issue was during check-in: although the hotel policy (clearly stated when we booked) mentioned that kids under 12 stay for free, we were asked to pay extra for our 6-year-old. The receptionist insisted the room couldn't accommodate an extra bed, which we were never informed about beforehand. This left us with no choice but to pay unexpected fees.

To make matters worse, we requested a late check-out since we had already entered our rooms at 3 PM on arrival and paid extra fees. We expected some flexibility. However, only one of our two rooms was granted a late check-out, and even that was only until 2 PM. The receptionist, Ahmed Farid, handled this very poorly and refused to accommodate our reasonable request, showing no sense of customer care.

Overall, the reception experience – from check-in to check-out – was extremely frustrating and unprofessional. While the hotel itself has potential, the way it is managed at the front desk leaves a lot to be desired. Sadly, this may be our last visit unless major improvements are made.

---

### 248. Dániel
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-06-15 · визит: 2025-06-03 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Hungary

Плюсы: Gyönyörű design. Kedves személyzet. Nagyon finom ételek, már az alap svédasztalos étteremben is nagyon jól főznek, de a 3 á la carte éttermet is mindenképp érdemes kipróbálni (a tartózkodásunk alatt mindegyiket 1x lehetett igénybe venni foglalás után). Az fő étteremben voltak ismétlődések az ételekben, de az egymást követő fő étkezéseknél mindig más volt a választék. Az all inclusive italválaszték is tökéletesen kielégítő volt, minden helyi alkohol és belelőlük készült koktél benne van az árban, a minőségibb "európai" italok felárasak. A medencék és a szálloda saját strandja nekünk tökéletesen elég volt a mindennapi strandoláshoz. Szépen rendbe tartják a szobákat. Jó esti programok vannak a hotelen belül is. Mi egy tengerre néző szobában voltunk, aminek köszönhetően minden nagyon közel volt, pillanatok alatt jutottunk le a strandra, étterembe, stb. ezt jó szívvel ajánljuk másoknak is.

Минусы: A szórakoztató személyzet megpróbál minden programra beszervezni, főleg a fő medence körül, de a strandon is működnek. Egy idő után ez fárasztó tudott lenni, ha pl aznap csak pihenni szeretne az ember és akkor is jönnek, hogy menjen velük ugrálni. Sajnos a napágyakhoz nem jár kis tábla, hogy te ma csak nyugit szeretnél, deha olvasol vagy látványosan pihensz akkor békén hagynak.

### 258. Eman
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **5.0** · опубликован: 2025-06-07 · визит: 2025-06-02 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Australia

Минусы: ⸻

Overall Experience:
The staff at this hotel are truly wonderful—polite, helpful, and consistently attentive. They appear genuinely happy in their work and always give their best effort, which adds greatly to the welcoming atmosphere.

Dining:
The chefs clearly take pride in what they do. The food is always plentiful, high quality, and beautifully presented with vibrant colors that make each meal feel special.

Accommodation & Facilities:
The rooms are clean and spacious. Both the beach and pool areas are well maintained, offering a pleasant environment for relaxation.

Areas for Improvement:
Unfortunately, the hotel is not particularly disability-friendly. Although our bathroom was designed for accessibility, the shower drainage was inadequate, causing water to overflow and soak the entire floor—a significant slip hazard.
Additionally, the pathways throughout the hotel include steep slopes and uneven surfaces, and many ramps are too steep to be considered safely accessible. Both indoor and outdoor tiles are overly polished, making them slippery and hazardous.

Entertainment:
The animation team could be more proactive in engaging guests. Evening entertainment is very limited and often underwhelming, so those looking for vibrant nightlife may be disappointed.

---

### 279. Claudia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-05-30 · визит: 2025-05-21 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Switzerland

**Best Hotel especially for families with small kids**

Best Hotel especially for families with small kids

Плюсы: Everything was perfect!
We had the most amazing 10 days at this hotel — I can’t recommend it enough. From the moment we arrived, the level of attention and care from the entire staff was outstanding. The facilities, rooms, and food were all fantastic, but what truly made our stay unforgettable was the exceptional customer service.

Everyone was so attentive and genuinely friendly, especially with my 18-month-old, who had an absolute blast. He especially loved the baby disco every evening — such a fun and thoughtful activity. It was heartwarming to see how kind and welcoming everyone was with him.

We were also really impressed by the management team, who regularly walked around to speak with guests and make sure everything was perfect. That personal touch made us feel truly valued and cared for.

We’re already looking forward to coming back!

### 281. Matthias
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **4.0** · опубликован: 2025-05-30 · визит: 2025-05-13 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Germany

**Nie wieder in dieses Hotel**

Nie wieder in dieses Hotel

Минусы: Das komplette Hotel ist mit sehr glatten Fliesen ausgestattet. Auch die Treppen, was einen Sturz verursachte mit Armbruch. Die Hotelärztin hat mich hervorragend behandelt, hat aber 1200,-€ gekostet. Ich wurde dann zum privates Krankenhaus gefahren, welches aber nur darauf aus war, meine Geldbörse zu leeren. Ein CT sollte 1400,-€ kosten und die Op ( die, wie sich in Deutschland herausstellte gar nicht notwendig ist) war auch gleich geplant.
Der Manager, den wir am nächsten Tag sprachen, wies alle Schuld von sich. Da wir zusätzlich noch Baulärm vom Nachbargrundstück hatten und auch keinen Zimmerservice hatten, bot uns einen Rabatt von 10% auf unsere Buchung an. Allerdings setzte er den Preis vorher hoch, so dass wir 200,-€ mehr zahlen sollten als die ursprüngliche Buchung.
Nie wieder in dieses Hotel!!!

---

### 282. Sylvan

### 283. Birgül
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2025-05-28 · визит: 2025-05-13 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Germany

**Sehr schönes und sauberes Hotel, Preis-Leistung ist top!**

Sehr schönes und sauberes Hotel, Preis-Leistung ist top!

Плюсы: Das Hotel ist sehr nah zum Flughafen, wodurch man nur eine kurze Anreise hat, vom Flughafenlârm bekommt man allerdings nichts mit. Die Zimmer und die Hotelanlage waren sehr sauber, das Personal war immer sehr bemüht den Aufenthalt so schön wie möglich zu machen. Liegen gab es genug sowohl an den Pools als auch am Strand.
Das Essen war sehr lecker und abwechslungsreich, insbesondere die Desserts mit Künefe. Auch die a-la-carte Restaurants waren sehr lecker. Wir durften alle einmal ausprobieren während unseres Aufenthalts.
Die Animateure (Moody, Sarina, Mohamed, Ela, Dj İka) waren auch sehr lieb und um unser Wohlergehen bemüht; Aktivitâten gab es reichlich, nicht zu viel aber auch nicht zu wenig. Die Unterhaltungen tagsüber und das Abendprogramm waren immer sehr lustig.
Der F&B Manager İbrahem und seine Kollegin haben sich jeden Tag mehrmals nach unserem Wohlergehen erkundigt und nachgefragt ob uns etwas fehlt. Das kennt man so in Hotels nicht, es war sehr schön einen direkten Ansprechpartner zu haben.
John von der Rezeption hat alle unsere Anliegen sehr schnell und zu unserer vollsten Zufriedenheit gelöst.

Alles in allem ist das Hotel sehr empfehlenswert. Wir werden wiederkommen!

---

### 284. Changhoon
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **2.0** · опубликован: 2025-05-26 · визит: 2025-05-24 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: South Korea

**시뷰 패밀리스위트 룸으로 이 호텔에서 가장 비싼 방 예약했는데 그라운드층 주더니 시뷰 맞냐 따지니 저기 멀리 바다 보인다고 장난침**

시뷰 패밀리스위트 룸으로 이 호텔에서 가장 비싼 방 예약했는데 그라운드층 주더니 시뷰 맞냐 따지니 저기 멀리 바다 보인다고 장난침

Плюсы: 시설과 사진빨 그리고 바다가 예쁨.

Минусы: 주차후 아무도 안 도와주다 짐 질질 끌고 로비에 다 도착하니 일부 도와줌.
리셉션체크인시 위챗 있냐 대뜸 물어봄. 중국인이라 그냥 판단해버림. 예약서에 코리안 다 나오는데. 이집트 놈들은 그냥 아예 안 봄. 아니면 알면서 그러는지도.
식당 몇개냐 소개해 달라니 계속해서 메인레스토랑 부페만 얘기해 무려 4차례나 식당이 그거 1개냐 물어봤는데 반복해 부페얘기만 함. 인터넷에서 여러개 봤다하니 이탈리안. 프렌치 등 얘기함
시뷰로 패밀리스위트 예약햇는데 완전 그라운층 줘 가보니 밖에 수영장지나다니는 사람들 다 지나다녀 프라이버시 전혀 없었고 뷰도 저 멀리 바다 가까스로 보이는데 시뷰라고 우겨 싸움. 강력하게 컴플레인하니 그제서야 바로 윗층으로 옮겨줌. 이렇게 2시간이 그냥 허비 됨. 다른 방 없으니 가든뷰로 가야된다하더니 계약위반이라 취소한다니 1층 더 높은 곳으로 바꿔줘 그제야 체크인 끝남. 그리고 이탈리안 레스토랑 7시로 예약해 왔더니 예약 안 돼 있다고 오락가락하다 겨우 앉음. 종업원들 중 일부도 그냥 중국인으로 단정하고 쉐쉐 니하오 하는 것들이 있음. 시설은 좋고 바다도 예쁘나 그냥 유럽 늙은이들만 받으려는 듯 좀 이상하고 싸한 느낌을 받음.

### 294. Laco
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-05-15 · визит: 2025-05-07 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Slovakia

Плюсы: Už pri príchode na hotel urobilo dobrý dojem na recepcii - posadili nas, priniesol vlhčené uteráky, uvítací drink. Služby hotela na vysokej úrovni. Strava fantastická, obmieňala sa nielen večere ako to býva v iných hoteloch v Egypte ale aj raňajky, obedy. Po celý deň fresh - jahody, mango, guava, pomaranč, jablko, ananas. Zo stavy bol obrovský výber, ale aj morské plody - krevety, kalamáre, kraby, ryby, údený losos, sušené hovädzie, suši atď, aj zákusky a koláče nielenže dobre vyzerali aj chutili ......... Výborná káva, kapučíno, laté, kokteily vo všetkých baroch. Cez deň nanuky, občerstvenie počas dňa - pizza, burgre, ovocie, čašníci chodili ako pri bazéne tak aj na pláži - výborný servis. Umelo vytvorená lagúna, vstup do mora na boso, more výborné na plávanie. Pochodil som veľmi veľa hotelov, ale tento má vysokú úroveň. Všade pekne čisto. Manželka má hystamínovú intoleranciu, tak kuchár chodil s ňou a hovoril z čoho je to pripravované. Manželka obľubuje polievky, vždy boli na výber dve, ale keď ich nemohla jesť, kuchár jej dokonca išiel pripraviť na počkanie polievku. Kiežby všetky hotely zo siete Jaz mali takúto úroveň.

---

### 314. Khalid
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2025-05-06 · визит: 2025-04-25 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: South Africa

**Had a wonderful time!**

Had a wonderful time!

Плюсы: We had an absolutely fantastic time at Casa Del Mar. From the moment we arrived, everything exceeded our expectations. The food was simply amazing with plenty of variety, fresh ingredients, and delicious flavors at every meal. The service was top-notch, and the staff were consistently polite, friendly, and willing to go the extra mile to help.

The animation team kept the vibe lively and fun throughout our stay with engaging activities and entertainment for all ages. A special shoutout to Haidy and John, who checked in on us regularly and made sure we were always well taken care of. They truly added a personal touch to our experience.

The hotel’s location is perfect, close to everything, and the rooms were clean, spacious, and comfortable. We couldn’t have asked for a better holiday. Highly recommend Casa Del Mar to anyone looking for a relaxing yet exciting stay in Hurghada. We’ll definitely be back!

Минусы: Nothing, it was great!

---

The perfect getaway! A stay to remember!

Плюсы: The hospitality is exceptional at the hotel. Everyone was extremely helpful! Eslam, Mohammed, Hassan, John and all of the staff were so accommodating with outstanding customer service! The animation team were fantastic! I particularly enjoyed the water aerobics and yoga everyday. The evening entertainment was also excellent. The room was modern and definitely 5star quality. The bed was very comfortable. The food was the best I have ever tasted at an all inclusive resort! They had fresh juices and a very extensive beverages list! I visited the Spa and this was also exceptional. I can’t wait to go back! I really loved my stay.

### 337. Aurélie
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-04-17 · визит: 2025-04-09 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: France

**Séjours au Paradis**

Séjours au Paradis

Плюсы: Nous avons passé une superbe semaine dans cet hôtel avec ma fille de 10 ans. Tout était parfait : l’accueil, la chambre spacieuse avec vue sur la mer, la plage privée, les différents restaurants et bars avec une nourriture délicieuse, ainsi que les boissons et les glaces à volonté. John a été notre interlocuteur principal, toujours présent et attentionné, même après notre retour en France. Un grand merci aussi à Hazim au restaurant et au monsieur du ménage de notre chambre, tous les deux adorables avec nous. En cas de problème de santé, la réception fait tout pour nous aider : il y a une clinique et un médecin disponibles 24h/24 dans l’hôtel, ainsi qu’une pharmacie juste en face. L’hôtel est neuf, très sécurisé, et le personnel est exceptionnel. Petit bémol : le Kids Club mériterait d’être un peu plus adapté aux grands enfants. Mais vraiment, nous reviendrons avec grand plaisir !

---

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-04-13 · визит: 2025-04-08 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Turkey

**Tekrar gelmeyi düşünderecek kadar iyiydi.**

Tekrar gelmeyi düşünderecek kadar iyiydi.

Плюсы: Mısır’a ilk gelişimizdi, ancak Jaz Elite Casa Del Mar Beach oteli sayesinde oldukça memnun kaldık. Odalar tertemizdi, yemek ve içecekler her şey dahil konseptine göre oldukça kaliteliydi. Ancak bu oteli gerçekten özel kılan şey, tüm personelin samimi, güler yüzlü ve misafirperver yaklaşımı. Herkes elinden gelenin en iyisini yaparak sizi evinizde gibi hissettirmeye çalışıyor. Kolay kolay aynı yere ikinci kez gitmeyen bir çift olarak, bu otel bizi tekrar gelmeye ikna etti. Tüm ekibe içten teşekkür ederiz.

Минусы: Wi-Fi kalitesi, yatakların ve yastıkların rahatlığı, otelin genelinde ve havuzbaşında alkol kalitesi daha iyi olabilirdi.

Le resort est magnifique, totalement conforme aux photos, la chambre est très confortable. Etant pointilleuse sur la nourriture, j’ai rarement vu un buffet aussi qualitatif, un vaste de choix de plats qui changent à chaque repas, petit déjeuner, déjeuner et dîner, fait avec des produits frais, avec des chefs sur place c’était incroyablement bon ! Je recommande à 200%

### 369. İrem
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-04-05 · визит: 2025-03-30 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Turkey

Плюсы: We just returned from a wonderful 6-day stay at Jaz Casa Del Mar Beach in Hurghada, and I can’t recommend it enough! From the moment we booked, everything went smoothly — especially thanks to John, who was incredibly helpful and supportive even before our arrival. He made sure all our questions were answered and that we felt truly welcome.

The hotel itself is beautiful and well-maintained, with a relaxing atmosphere and a lovely beach area. The staff were always friendly, attentive, and professional. We never encountered a single issue during our stay — everything was clean, organized, and ran perfectly.

We were also celebrating my fiancé’s birthday during the trip, and the staff made it extra special. They went above and beyond to make us feel celebrated, which meant a lot to both of us. It’s rare to find such genuine hospitality, and it really made our experience unforgettable. Thank you again and see you soon!

---

### 374. Anna
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2025-04-03 · визит: 2025-03-22 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Germany

**War im Großen und Ganzen ein netter Urlaub**

War im Großen und Ganzen ein netter Urlaub

Плюсы: Das Zimmer war bei Ankunft sehr dreckig, Haare in der Dusche, Toilette stinkt, Babybett vollgestaubt, braune Flecken im Waschbecken etc. Wir wussten natürlich, dass die Hygienestandarts anders als in Deutschland sind, trotzdem war es eine Enttäuschung bei Anreise. Das Essen ist leider ein wenig geschmacklos, im a la carte Restaurant gibt es dagegen besseres Essen. Die Cocktails sind auch sehr wässrig. Ansonsten ist die Lage Top, tolle Pools, alles sehr großzügig, privater großer Strand. Die meisten Mitarbeiter sind sehr nett und kinderlieb. Die Poollhandtücher werden täglich gewechselt wenn man sie außen an die Türe hängt, das wurde ein paar Mal vergessen. Darauf wollten wir neue Handtücher an der Rezeption holen. Die Mitarbeiter waren davon offensichtlich genervt, wortlos wurden uns neue Handtücher hingeklatscht.

### 379. Maarten
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2025-03-31 · визит: 2025-02-09 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Netherlands

**Excellent place to relax and unwind and enjoy the sun**

Excellent place to relax and unwind and enjoy the sun

Плюсы: I spent a week at this resort with the main purpose to charge the battery. The resort is brand new and has everything you look for when you're searching for an all inclusive hotel with decent facilities. In the week I've been there, I've seen everything in and around the hotel. The best points are:
- Easy access from the airport to the hotel. When you get back make sure the driver delivers you at the right part of the airport. The airport of Hurghada itself is quite bad.
- The hotel itself lies at a strip of hotels with renovated streets and shops, which makes it pleasant to walk around there a little bit.
- The buffets are outstanding, it offers so much enjoyable food. Especially the deserts were delicious.
- The pools were a bit cold at the time of the year I went there, but are large and also the beach is near.
- Easy access to fitness and wellness facilities, which were perfect.
- Booking trips through the hotel is also easy to do.
- Friendly staff, and when you get there, leave some money for the cleaners, they are too often overlooked.

Минусы: The two main points of improvement for this hotel are:
- Wifi connections are a bit slow and not easily accessible.
- GOOD coffee. I know it can be expensive for a hotel: but please invest in good machines when you offer 5 star services to your clients.

---

### 408. Ulrike
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-03-09 · визит: 2025-02-27 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Germany

**Einfach hervorragend !**

Einfach hervorragend !

Плюсы: We've been on a dive safari and choose this Hotel as its situated close to the airport(but not noisy at all !). From the first moment we felt very welcome. As our plane had a massive delay we got picked up at the airport after midnight by a hotel limousine. The driver was waiting for us with a good visible sign. Check in went fast and we got offered an early little breakfast and welcome drink at the lobby Bar Uno.
Already before our arrival John from the Hotel reception had contacted us via WhatsApp to assist us with our arrival.He is a real gem, very professional, always helpful and friendly. The day of our departure to the dive safari we even got a late check out without any charge.
The Hotel itself is beautiful! Modern design with a warm atmosphere. The rooms are spacious and very clean, bathroom in a stylish modern design with good water pressure. The restaurant and pool bars are great, food outstanding and abundant and the service attentive. We loved the adults area, as its so quiet and good to relax. There are heated and unheated pools, all cristal clear and clean.
After our dive safari John from reception answered prompt on our request for a day guests stay as our departure was late in the evening. We even got a reduction as a part of our group had to leave already at midday.
All in all we highly recommend Jay casa Del Mar Hotel with its great design and facilities, delicious food and outstanding service.
Our special thanks goes to John from the reception as he's providing us a very professional personal care.

---

### 409. Angela
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-03-09 · визит: 2025-03-01 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Austria

**Schöner, erholsamer Urlaub mit tollem Service, einem lieben Team und lecker Essen!**

Schöner, erholsamer Urlaub mit tollem Service, einem lieben Team und lecker Essen!

Плюсы: Das Hotel ist wirklich super schön. Es ist sehr weitläufig und man hat nie das Gefühl, dass es überlaufen wäre. Die Zimmer sind sehr geräumig.
Besonders hervorzuheben ist das Personal. Sowohl das gesamte Animationsteam, als auch das Hotelpersonal selbst ist so freundlich und zuvorkommend. Man äußert einen Wunsch, u er wird in kürzester Zeit erfüllt. Auch Josef, der stellvertretende Direktor und sein Team bemühen sich sehr um das Wohl der Gäste und gehen jeden Tag von Gast zu Gast und fragen, was man noch tun kann, damit es uns Gästen besser geht. Das Essen war übrigens unglaublich - man sollte ausreichend Zeit für das Sport Programm einplanen…
Es ist wirklich ein ganz tolles Hotel. Uns hat es an gar nichts gefehlt. Wir kommen gern wieder!

L'hotel est bien entretenu, et très bien situé, sur le front de mer avec plage privée, mais aussi belle promenade en ville pas loin du musée. La nourriture est très bonne et variée, à part la pâtisserie qui est bien présentée visuellement, mais pas trop au goût.

Минусы: Les animations qui sont très " bruyantes " et se passent autour de la piscine, mais on en profite bien dans la chambre qui est avec vue sur mer ( au loin) mais aussi sur la piscine au premier plan ! Mais en parlant à la réception, j'ai changé 2 fois de chambre. Et sur la plage, vous profitez aussi de l'animation de l'hotel à côté ! En fait venue pour travailler et se reposer et effectivement ce n'est pas l'endroit idéal, c'est le seul point noir.

---

### 415. Mihwa
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2025-03-05 · визит: 2025-02-04 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: South Korea

### 420. Pia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-03-03 · визит: 2025-02-25 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Switzerland

Ich habe einer meiner entspannendsten und schönsten Urlaub verbracht. Ich komme wieder in dieses Hotel. War traumhaft.

Минусы: Es war alles wunderschön. Könnte nicht besser sein.

### 434. Stephen
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2025-02-27 · визит: 2025-02-17 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: United Kingdom

**Great winter sunshine break**

Great winter sunshine break

Плюсы: the hotel staff were perfect, nothing was too much trouble. we travel with a wheelchair and everybody helped when we needed it, in fact it was difficult sometimes to stop them from helping. There was plenty of variety in the restaurant and although all the staff were lovely, we had some very special treatment from chef Hani, who made some very nice dishes for my wife, he couldn't have been more helpful, a very nice man who it was a pleasure to meet. I completed my Padi training whilst on holiday and although I am in my late 60s the dive team made it easy for me to complete the course. The boat trip was also perfect because although we have a wheelchair all of the team helped my wife onto the beach, sometimes carrying her in the chair between boats, if it wasn't for the staff especially Mani, our guide we would not have been able to go on the trip.
thank you for a great holiday

Минусы: Although my wife is a wheelchair user she can manage some walking with help, on the whole the hotel is disabled friendly, with lifts and ramps, the toilets were not as accessible as they could be.

---

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-02-18 · визит: 2025-02-10 · компания: Couple · номер: Family Suite · страна: Germany

Плюсы: Vom Hotel bis zum Animationsteam haben 5 Sterne wohl aus verdient. Die Animateure Liza, Ella, Moody und Haya waren immer für einen Spaß zuhaben und sorgten für ein Lächeln in unseren Gesichtern. Uns wurde viel Spaß bereitet was den Urlaub einfach nicht mehr toppen konnte. Wir haben ein grosses Dankeschön auszusprechen an das tolle Team vom Animations Team bis hin zum Service-Bereich, sowohl Reinigung wie auch Restaurants und Bars. Wir wurden immer mit einem Lächeln bedient und angesprochen. Vielen herzlichen Dank auch an die Küche, die eine vielseitige Auswahl anbot. Unser erster Urlaub hier war also perfekt und wir freuen uns aufs nächste Mal.

### 460. Xinyu
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **3.0** · опубликован: 2025-02-12 · визит: 2025-02-03 · компания: Couple · номер: Family Suite · страна: China

**入住感受非常不好 不会再次入住 也不会推荐给身边朋友入住**

入住感受非常不好 不会再次入住 也不会推荐给身边朋友入住

Плюсы: 酒店风景不错 装修有格调 海水干净

Минусы: 员工素质低，对待亚裔无微笑服务。我们在餐厅用餐6.7次，没有一次有任何一名员工来问好或者引导，没有问我们需要什么饮品（我们都会说英语，并不是因为我们不会说英语才不问好），而对欧美游客则是击掌攀谈，全程微笑。在楼道内于员工面对面路过的时候，员工对我们的路过熟视无睹，在我们主动微笑问好的情况下，依然非常冷漠回复，只遇到过一次客房服务人员在楼道内与我们交谈问好，积极热情，只有那一位员工这样做了。房间淋浴下水不通畅，洗着洗着脚面就会被水淹没，联系工作人员来修理，工作人员响应速度慢，且所谓的修理只是将喷头不停的喷向下水口，实则没有任何意义，并且告诉我们他们觉得这个下水速度是正常的，整个酒店所有房间的下水都是如此，在我们强烈要求下才给我们换了房间，新的房间下水比较正常。另外，泳池旁的全部躺椅以及海边沙滩的部分躺椅都是需要单独付费使用的，虽然价格不贵，但作为一个价格并不便宜的所谓全包酒店来说，（对比其他酒店）这个做法确实太小气了。餐食选择很多，但口味更偏向当地口味及西式口味。整个小海湾的沙滩是与隔壁酒店共享，以中间为线，分成两半。退房的时候，有位经理对我们房间下水道的事情表示了歉意，但是我觉得不如尽快改善员工服务，退房时的口头歉意可能并不会改善住客对酒店的看法。

---

### 494. Roxanne
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-01-09 · визит: 2024-12-28 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: United Kingdom

Плюсы: Amazing stay from start to finish. Staff couldn't do more to provide one of the best services we have ever received in a hotel.
Room: had a two bedroom suite side sea view direct access from balcony few steps and on the beach. Beds were super compfy!! Nice and quiet. We didn't hear any music from neighbouring hotel which we did read about on some reviews so not sure what that is about but we didnt hear anything. Mini bar with free soft drinks. Water is in glass bottles but filtered and changed so fresh every day. Dressing gowns. Beach towels all included to use.
Food was excellent and waiting staff couldn't do enough to make sure drinks were refilled and we had everything we needed. Buffet restaurants had lots of great choice with a bbq area also.
The other a la carte restraunts were great and different options to what was in the buffet restraunt was nice having the table service with the food.
Drinks:cocktails freshly made with slices of fruit we couldn't fault them. Wine and beers were drinkable and can't complain about that either.
Beach: private beach sectioned off. Bar on beach was great and waiter service to the deck chairs which was lovely and relaxing. Lots of choice of pools.
Overall had an amazing stay and would book again.

---

### 497. Veronika
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2025-01-06 · визит: 2024-11-25 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Switzerland

**Gutes Chill**

Gutes Chill

Плюсы: Das Restaurantpersonal war extrem freundlich. Das All-inclusive-Angebot ist grossartig. Die Rezeption sieht wunderschön aus, und die Drinks sind ebenfalls von hoher Qualität. All-inclusive gibt es nicht nur im Hauptrestaurant, sondern auch in kleineren Restaurants in der Nähe der kleineren Pools.

Минусы: Unser Zimmer war nah an Energie-Generator. Es war zu laut. Klar wurde uns angeboten, Zimmer zu wechseln. Das war aber etwas mühsam, weil wir davor viel unterwegs waren und nur wenige Tage im Hotel hatten. Daher hat der Zimmerwechsel im Verhältnis zu Aufenthaltsdauer viel Zeit und Energie konsumiert. Jeden Abend gab es laute Musik bis 23.00.

---

### 498. Dina
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **5.0** · опубликован: 2025-01-06 · визит: 2024-12-22 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Switzerland

Минусы: Wenn man eine Family Suite bucht, sollte man nicht automatisch davon ausgehen, dass man einen Meerblick erhält, selbst wenn in der Beschreibung „Seaview“ steht. Leider sind die meisten Ausblicke nicht besonders schön, was sehr enttäuschend ist, vor allem, wenn einem der Ausblick wichtig ist und man dafür extra bezahlt hat.

Die Family Suite mit „Garden View“, die wir bekommen haben, bietet tatsächlich nur einen Blick auf das Restaurant, und zu den Mittags- und Abendzeiten ist der Geruch vom Grill deutlich wahrnehmbar.

Zwar war das Personal an der Rezeption freundlich, doch sie konnten uns keine zufriedenstellende Lösung anbieten – weder eine kostenlose Stornierung noch einen Zimmerwechsel mit Meerblick.

---

### 499. Georgia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2025-01-06 · визит: 2024-12-26 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: United Kingdom

**A fantastically relaxing hotel with great staff.**

A fantastically relaxing hotel with great staff.

Плюсы: The staff were really friendly, attentive and always ready to help. From house keeping to bar staff, restaurant waiters and animation team, the service was exceptional; and boy, do they work hard! Nothing was too big an ask. The staff made an effort to get to know us as a family; and we were truly sad to leave.
This place is really relaxing. The quality and variety of the food in the buffet was outstanding. The bar staff were attentive and always ensured you didn't get dehydrated.
My daughter can struggle with being away from home and busy hectic places, but even she found our holiday to be very relaxing and enjoyable.
The hotel is extremely clean and well maintained. I celebrated a birthday whilst staying at the hotel and the staff went above and beyond to make it so memorable, providing a congratulatory note from the team, a delicious cake from the kitchen and and by dressing the bed with flowers and foilage.

Минусы: There is really nothing much to fault our wonderful holiday here as it was pretty much perfect - thank you. Installing a few infrared heaters in the outside splash bar and aqua restaurant for the evenings dining and entertainment would have improved the experience in winter. The a la carte restaurants were difficult to book and always full, so we only experienced two of these.

**Schön und gut - für ein All-Inclusive Resort**

Schön und gut - für ein All-Inclusive Resort

Плюсы: Gute Lage, Ausstattung, freundlicher Service.
Alkoholhaltige Cocktails (ägyptischer Alkoholverschnitt, der sich für ein paar Tage gerade ertragen lässt) sind im All-Inclusive enthalten.
Über die Weihnachtstage wurde viel für eine weihnachtliche Stimmung getan (Musik, Glühwein, Dekoration) und Heiligabend gab es ein großes Event zum Abendessen.

Минусы: Das Essen (Hauptrestaurant/Buffet) sah oft spektakulär aus, war aber höchstens mäßig.
Die im Hotel Angebotenen Dienstleistungen wie Spa oder auch Windsurfen oder Exkusionen sind exorbitant. Stattdessen kann man per Uber (~100 EGP) zur Marina fahren und dort mit einen deutlich besseren Deal bekommen

Минусы: C'est peut-être un détail, mais le réseau informatique interne à l'hôtel pour les réservations aux divers restaurant est une catastrophe, beaucoup trop lent. Il ne correspond pas au standard actuel de performance. Mais, heureusement quand on est en vacances, c'est un peu moins important.

### 510. Lujia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2024-12-26 · визит: 2024-12-19 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: United Kingdom

Плюсы: Pools are clean and nice. The hotel arranged daily resort activities for guest which is warm. Most of staff in restaurants and bars here are friendly and never ask for tips.

Минусы: On the day leaving, we reject the offer from reception to call taxi for us. They respond by blocking our taxi from entering the hotel gate to pick us up from the lobby (we drove in here once so we know it’s possible to drive in). And there isn’t a ‘early breakfast’ provided as stated in hotel info. The lobby bar waiter only give us six small pastries for breakfast on the day leaving, in a perfunctory manner (he fed us with postures like feeding dogs, so disrespectful).

**У меня был прекрасный день рождения. С огромным тортом из полотенцев и море лепестков из роз.**

У меня был прекрасный день рождения. С огромным тортом из полотенцев и море лепестков из роз.

Плюсы: Великолепный коллектив современный отель чистота. Дизайнер большое спасибо Ларисе знаток своего дела. Если у вас будет желание справить свой день рождения рекомендую. Я получила массу удовольствия все прекрасно. Великолепная кухня,натуральные соки. Если я вернусь в Нургаду только в этот отель.

Минусы: Первое впечатление бывает ошибочным самолёт из Анталии прилетает ночью и приходиться начевать в комнате для отдыха на диване. Но это по моему во всех отелях

### 518. Nataliia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-12-16 · визит: 2024-12-07 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Ukraine

Плюсы: Готель дуже гарний, дуже компактний, по міркам Єгипетських готелей його можно назвати Бутік Готель. Дуже смачно на шведській лінії, дуже гарний та уважний персонал. Номери свіжі і красиві, зручна постіль, прибирання чудове. Неймовірний краєвид з басейну на море, картинка тішить око, на самому пляже лежаки не дуже зручні тому ми проводили час на басейні. Є тренажерний зал але він маленький, не вистачає хоч би декілька додаткових тренажерів на все тіло !
Хоч цей отель і не Adults only, дітей в готелі майже не має. Сподобалось що є один цілодобовий бар з усіма доступними напоями.
Якщо ви любите полежати на басейні на зручних лежаках, якщо ви любите смачно поїсти, якщо ви любите невеликі території щоб не ходити один кілометр на сніданок, то я однозначно рекомендую цей готель !

Минусы: Все сподобалося!

### 525. Jay
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **5.0** · опубликован: 2024-12-10 · визит: 2024-12-07 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: United Kingdom

**1st and only time I will stay at a Jaz Hotel- in fact Hurgharda for that matter**

1st and only time I will stay at a Jaz Hotel- in fact Hurgharda for that matter

Плюсы: The pillows on the bed were comfortable ! regarding the 99 % male staff I would say 97% were polite and respectful. The hotel was extremely clean and tidy and aesthetically pleasing on the eye.

Минусы: I didn't like being woken up by the cleaning staff at 7am screaming and shouting to each other as they went about there job. My room was alongside a building site with disc cutters, beeping reversing trucks and jackhammers going off during the day. I did not like my "a la carte" meal experience with 1 choice of a starter and main course being only meat or fish ! The red wine was kept in an ice bucket ! once I had warmed the vino via cupping the glass in my hands for a good 7 mins... I wished I hadn't, also strangely I was offered an espresso coffee twice, immediately after my starter and when I declined "twice" the look I received was if I was a madman. I did not like the fact that I could only log in 1 device onto wifi and if I needed to add a second device I had to go to reception EVERY day. The buffet breakfast food was sub par compared to the liveaboard I had been on before my short stay, there was not a single coffee cup that was not chipped... at least that I could find.

### 530. Anna
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-12-05 · визит: 2024-11-28 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Latvia

**Наш вкусный отпуск. :)
Гостеприимство, комфорт и отличный сервис.**

Наш вкусный отпуск. :)
Гостеприимство, комфорт и отличный сервис.

Плюсы: Наш отпуск в этом отеле был просто великолепным, и во многом это заслуга непревзойденного сервиса и атмосферы гостеприимства!

Кулинарные впечатления превзошли все ожидания. Разнообразие блюд поражало своим качеством и вкусом. Особенно восхитили креветки, стейк в ресторане Помодоро, жареные баклажаны, десерт в виде гнезда с мороженным, а так же свежевыжатые соки – каждый глоток манго был настоящим тропическим удовольствием. Что особенно приятно, соки были доступны не только во время основных приемов пищи, но и на пляже, что делало отдых еще более комфортным.

Анимационная программа была настолько деликатной, что создавала атмосферу непринужденного веселья. Отдельные слова благодарности – Лизе, чья приветливость и внимание к деталям заслуживают высочайшей оценки.

Номера производили впечатление современных, стильных и очень уютных. Особенно порадовали удобные матрасы, которые обеспечивали превосходный комфортный сон после насыщенного дня.

Расположение отеля – это отдельный восторг! Он находится на одном из красивейших променадов Хургады, где буквально на каждом шагу множество очаровательных магазинчиков. Этот нюанс добавил нашему отдыху дополнительные краски и возможности для приятных прогулок и шопинга.

Руководство отеля демонстрирует редкостную заботу о гостях. Ежедневные личные беседы на пляже и около бассейна, а так же еженедельные встречи перед ужином, где они лично интересуются впечатлениями, говорят о высоком уровне сервиса. Наши небольшие пожелания были моментально услышаны и тут же реализованы.

Особую признательность хочется выразить менеджеру Ибрагиму – его профессионализм, теплота и внимание превратили наш отпуск в незабываемое путешествие. Такие сотрудники – настоящее золотое сокровище отеля, которое делает его по-настоящему выдающимся.

Мы искренне благодарны за этот потрясающий отдых и с радостью рекомендуем этот отель всем, кто ценит качественный сервис, комфорт и гостеприимство! Обязательно вернёмся ещё!

Минусы: Если быть предельно честными, наш отпуск был все равно идеальным, и критические замечания носят сугубо индивидуальный характер.

Единственный нюанс – это вода в номере. Отель придерживается экологичной концепции и использует систему очистки соленой воды, что, безусловно, заслуживает уважения. Это часть их философии заботы об окружающей среде. Однако для моего личного вкуса вода казалась немного жестковатой. Признаюсь честно – мы предпочитали наслаждаться свежевыжатыми соками или время от времени приобретать воду в близлежащих магазинчиках.

Подчеркну, что этот малозначительный момент с водой ни в коей мере не испортил общее впечатление от превосходного отдыха. Порой такие мелочи даже добавляют путешествию особый шарм индивидуальности и личного опыта.

---

### 538. Iryna
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-11-27 · визит: 2024-11-12 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Italy

**Bel soggiorno,ma con qualche piccola pecca.**

Bel soggiorno,ma con qualche piccola pecca.

Плюсы: La posizione centralissima per passeggiare la sera.
Camere e struttura in generale pulite,ordinate e curate in minimi dettagli.
Il personale gentilissimo,sorridente,amichevole e pronti per soddisfare qualsiasi esigenza.
Il ristorante centrale aveva una vasta scelta di cibo,ma purtroppo ogni sera la stessa cosa,per cui optammo a mangiare, quando si poteva, nei ristoranti à la carte,con ottimo cibo.
L’animazione è sempre presente ma non invadente.
Ringraziamo Ika,Mo,Lisa,Tina,Elisabeth e Billy per questi giorni. Ce ne siamo andati piangendo…

Минусы: Le pecche più grandi sono tre:
Per avere dei lettini sulla piscina ti devi svegliare alle 7 del mattino,altrimenti rimangono quelli dove il sole non arriva,sono davvero pochi a confronto le persone presenti in hotel. Potrebbero aggiungerne altri,visto che è riscaldata una sola piscina.
Un altro problema era il dopocena: lo spettacolo comincia alle 20:45 quando le persone mangiano ancora,e il tutto finisce molto presto. Dopo le 22 non c’era nulla da fare. L’animazione parla con le persone a tavolo(mai visto una cosa così) con un po’ di musica fino le 23,e tutti a dormire. Se vuoi ballare,devi andare nei locali fuori.. non c’è una sala giochi,con un tavolo da biliardo ad esempio… per cui molto tranquillo e spesso noioso (almeno per noi)
E la SPA: su 4 massaggi fatti, solo il mio è piaciuto. Il resto della famiglia ha lamentano seriamente. I soldi spesi non valgono, purtroppo. Evitate.. è piccola e per nulla rilassante.

---

### 547. Павел
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-11-21 · визит: 2024-10-01 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Russia

**Огромная благодарность мужчине который уберал номер.**

Огромная благодарность мужчине который уберал номер.

Плюсы: Понравилось все. Хороший чисты номер с двумя санузлами. Вид с балкона ( можно наблюдать как солнце встает утром). Чистое белье и полотенца. Много подушек разных, можно выбрать на какой удобно спать. Питание разнообразное. Любезный персонал в ресторане, спасибо им большое. Территория не большая, но ухоженная. Небольшие коралловые конгломераты на небольшой глубине с фауной. Даже иглобрюха видела. Так что маску берите и обувь для караулов это расширит ваш пляжный отдых. Еще хочу отметить музыкантов на ужине. Очень круто вечером услышать скрипку и саксофон.

Минусы: Персонал на ресепшене, простите, как «амебы». В моем случае я оставалась со своими проблемами после обращения, а то и вообще дезинформируют.

---

### 548. Nicholas
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-11-21 · визит: 2024-11-15 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: United Kingdom

Плюсы: The beach was nice, sea beautiful.
Buffet food selections and cakes, the waiters were friendly.
Hotel in good condition, all staff very friendly and helpful.
Despite the manager saying the hotel was fully booked, it never felt crowded. Plenty of sunbeds, always able to get food and find places to sit.
Nice wildlife, fish in the sea, corals not far, cute cat occasionally showed up at dinner time.

Минусы: The waiters didn't come to all the sunbeds on the beach. We had to get our own drinks, such is life.
In-between meals were nowhere near as good as the food at main buffet times.
Late breakfast was not good, no muffins sadly despite being in menu, lots of dry bread rolls with no butter.
It said all pools were heated, but the adult only pool was remarkably cold.

---

### 549. Ch
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-11-19 · визит: 2024-11-10 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Switzerland

Auf der Karte an den Bars sind Getränke aufgeführt die es nicht gibt (Redbull)

### 551. Mira
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-11-16 · визит: 2024-11-06 · компания: Couple · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Switzerland

**Visszajönnék mindenképpen, de pár információt jó tudni előtte :)**

Visszajönnék mindenképpen, de pár információt jó tudni előtte :)

Плюсы: Jó minőségű szép hangulatos hotel! Megvan az 5* érzés. Egyiptomban úgy gondolom, hogy kiváló választás. Finom ételek, italok. (Különleges sütemények!) A hotelnek vízszűrőrendszere van. Az alkoholos italok gyengébb minőségűek. Jó fekvésű szállás, viszont nem a tengerpart a hangsúlyos. Nagyrészt kedves mosolygós személyzet alap angol és német tudással legalább (többi nyelvekről nem tudok) - ellenben sokszor lehetett érzékelni a belső konfliktusaikat a bárokban.
1 hét tartózkodással 1x ehettünk az egyik a la carte étteremben is. Sajnos a “drágább” ételek, pl homár még így is felárasok voltak. A többi étel nem extra, de a hangulat és a kiszolgálás miatt megérte egyszer ott enni.
Mosás 15€! Egyiptomban más szállodàkhoz képest nagyon jó áron van!

Минусы: Kevés konkrét információt kaptunk. A papíron és az internetes felületen sem szerepeltek. Sok részletet úgy kellett a helyszínen kideríteni. (Pl az a la carte étteremben csak fent tudtuk meg, hogy mi van benne az all inclusiveban, pedig a menük mindegyike online is elérhető volt, beárazva. Már amikor… mert az internet nagyon lassú vagy nincs is!!! Dolgozni ne gyere ide, csak ha saját internetet hozol. :D az étlapokat a google chrome letölti. Safari esélytelen.
Nem tetszett, hogy mindig valaki odajött hozzánk. Ha kedvességből is, de egy idő után már inkább nyomasztó volt. Többször feküdtünk a strandon és csak olvasni szerettünk volna, mégis kényszerített beszélgetést kellett folytatni pl. az animátorokkal. Akkor is ha pl. szóltunk, hogy csak olvasni szeretnénk. Nem érzékelték, ki az aki nyitott a kezdeményezésre.
A szobánk (deluxe) szuper volt, bár nem vagyok biztos benne, hogy azt kaptuk amit kértem. A 2 helység (4 személyre szólt :D) de különösen hasznos volt, mert a klíma nagyon hangos alváshoz, ezért a másik szobában kapcsoltuk csak be. A szoba sajnos a konyha mellett helyezkedett el. A személyzeti folyosón minden éjszaka hallani lehetett a zajongást, csapkodást, ami elég zavaró volt sajnos :(
A zenék nagyon egyhangúak voltak. A Modern Talkingtól a Brother Louie-t szerintem jó darabig most nem akarom hallani. 😄

---

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-11-15 · визит: 2024-09-10 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Belgium

Superbe appart la nourriture

### 555. Марина
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-11-12 · визит: 2024-11-05 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Russia

**Не первый отель сети Jaz. В каждом чувствуется формат, единый стиль и качество. Было бы здорово иметь некую карту гостя**

Не первый отель сети Jaz. В каждом чувствуется формат, единый стиль и качество. Было бы здорово иметь некую карту гостя

Плюсы: Отель близок к аэропорту, 6 км. Без проблем можно взять так и и через 15-20 минут вы в отеле. Номера освежили, сам отель особенно рестораны после ремонта.
Прекрасная еда, разнообразно. Много фруктов, соков, море продуктов. Персонал вежливый. К ребенку все относились с большим вниманием, он был в восторге.
Хорошая анимация, детский клуб.
В отеле есть тихие места, есть тусовочные. Каждый найдет уголок по душе.
Дарц, пляжный волейбол, водное поло, и пр игры- почти всегда играли. Довольно много активных молодых людей которые включаются в развлечения.
Сносный спорт зал, 2 беговые дорожки - хотя вечернюю пробежку при желании можно сделать на улице-широкий безопасный променад сразу за главными воротами отеля

Минусы: Только одно, что не под властно отелю - отсутствие рифа((( Но об этом знали заранее, поэтому брали туры снорклинга

더 머물고 싶어요.
