### 564. Michaela
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2024-11-03 · визит: 2024-10-26 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Austria

**Es war wunderbar, bis auf die Abzocke deshalb würde ich in dieses Hotel nicht mehr fahren.**

Es war wunderbar, bis auf die Abzocke deshalb würde ich in dieses Hotel nicht mehr fahren.

Плюсы: Das Essen, Personal und Service war extrem freundlich und nett. Die Köche einfach Spitze. Auf Allergien sofort toll reagiert. Daumen HOCH. Mehr geht nicht!!!!!!

Минусы: Was leider ein großes Problem war, war die Lautstärke der Musik vom anderen Hotel. Leider konnte man sich dann am Meer/ Strand nicht mehr Aufhalten da es ein Wahnsinn war wie laut die die Musik aufgedreht hatten. Das gleich war dann auch in den Zimmern zu hören. Das muss doch mit den anderen Hotels abgeklärt werden das dies nicht so extrem extrem laut geht. Weiteres finde ich es eine Abzocke das man auf den guten Liegen mit Auflage bezahlen muss. Und am Strand nur harte durchgelegene Liegen sind. Wir haben auch 2 Ausflüge gebucht einmal Safari und einmal Delphine und Schnorcheln. Es war schön aber man bezahlt immer alles doppelt, das finde ich eine Frechheit. Und beim Schnorcheln war wirklich alles toll leider zum Schluss das hat uns sehr geärgert der Fotograf war ja mit hat alles fotografiert und dann sagt er für 100 Fotos per Whats App würden 140 Euro kosten. Bei aller LIEBE das ist eine Frechheit hoch drei. Wo man weiß wieviel die Menschen in so einen Land verdienen und dann so eine Abzocke das geht nicht und ich hatte mich auch beschwert im Hotel und an der Tauchschule aber das war jeden egal. Aber ich zahle doch nicht so einen Preis für nur ein paar Fotos.

Auch die Massagen gegenüber anderen Hotels sind eine Frechheit. Der Preis ist der Hammer. Ich gehe jede Woche in Österreich massieren und zahle die Hälfte. Das aber alles mit Rechnung und ausgebildeten Personal. Kenne ich jetzt von zb Hilton oder Steigenberger anders. Sehr schade.

---

### 566. Carmen
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-10-31 · визит: 2024-09-28 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Germany

**Schönes Hotel mit sehr gutem Service**

Schönes Hotel mit sehr gutem Service

Плюсы: Schönes Hotel direkt am Strand gelegen. Unsere beiden Zimmer mit Verbindungstür und 2 Bädern waren sauber, die Minibar wurde jeden Tag aufgefüllt.
Alle Mitarbeiter stets sehr freundlich, das Essen lecker 😋, oft sogar mit Livemusik. Einmal haben wir a la carte gegessen. Danke nochmal an den lustigen Kellner.
Abends gab es jeden Tag eine kleine Show am Pool. Einmal die Woche eine Strandparty. Die hat uns super gefallen👍. Tagsüber sorgt das Animationsteam für einige Aktivitäten. Liegen haben wir immer am Pool bekommen. Vor dem Hotel gibt es eine Promenade. Zu empfehlen ist dort der Barbier. Ein Männerhaarschnitt kostet 10 Euro.👍
Noch ein Tipp: Wir haben ein Taxi zum Flughafen benötigt. Gebucht hier über Booking, hat nicht mal 5 Euro gekostet, der Fahrer war überpünktlich, das Auto ordentlich, die Kommunikation nach der Buchung dann über Whatsapp. Einfach perfekt. 👍👍👍

Минусы: Wir wurden mehrfach vom Animationsteam angesprochen, mit deren Hilfe sofort eine Bewertung für das Hotel zuschreiben.

Das mach ich immernoch selbst!!!

### 571. Guzel
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-10-29 · визит: 2024-10-21 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Germany

**Great and clean place with good food and location**

Great and clean place with good food and location

Плюсы: We stayed for one week at the end of October. The location is perfect—only 10 minutes by taxi from the Hurghada airport. The room was clean and big enough. There were many storage places, a safe, a teapot, and a fridge. The double bed was big and very comfortable. The daily room cleaning service was excellent, always providing us with new towels and water, which was a reassuring sign of the hotel's commitment to hygiene and comfort.

The hotel staff was almost always friendly and supportive. We went to the spa twice, which was also a lovely experience. We found it a bit pricy (70EUR per person for 50 minutes of massage). However, the quality there was excellent. We also tried windsurfing, which is advertised on the hotel's beach. I highly recommend it. The entertaining programs in the evening were also exciting.

The hotel's dining options were a highlight of our stay. The food in the restaurant was consistently delicious, with a variety of grilled meats available for lunch and dinner. We had the opportunity to dine at three of the hotel's Grill, Mediterranean, and Italian restaurants, and we were impressed by the quality of the food and service at each one.

The beach was clean, and the sea entrance was very comfortable (with sand). The hotel also has a shisha corner with various tastes for a small price ($7 only).

Overall, we are very satisfied with our stay there!

Минусы: The water bottles were made from glass, and they refilled them every time the room was cleaned. However, the bottles themselves and the bottle caps were dirty, which raises the question of whether they cleaned them or not.

The room was a bit old and looked less sharp than in the photos.

During our stay, many flies annoyed us while we were sunbathing, eating, and walking in the hotel area.

We love playing chess, but there was only one board for the whole hotel, so we played only once...

---

**An unforgettable stay and would definitely recommend this hotel to others!**

An unforgettable stay and would definitely recommend this hotel to others!

Плюсы: Had an amazing one week stay at this hotel, did not want to leave! Staff were lovely and really take care of you and do what they can to make your stay as comfortable and convenient as possible. Food was always delicious with plenty of variety. The room was spacious and always cleaned well, bathroom was big and always stocked up. The promenade where the hotel is located has a fab range of shops to buy souvenirs or go for an evening stroll. Shisha at the hotel was nice and very cheap - Abd Elazem really looked after us. Overall, don't have any complaints or can't fault this hotel!

Минусы: Wi-Fi connection wasn't always the strongest but I did not mind too much as I had an E-Sim anyway which worked well. Would have loved to have been able to go swimming later in the evenings but understandable why all the pools close at sunset.

Плюсы: La plage, la variété et qualité des repas, le personnel, la propreté, les infrastructures, les différentes piscines avec ambiances variées (calme ou animée selon la piscine), la qualité des soins en spa.

Минусы: Nous avions 2 chambres et dans l’une des chambres le service de ménage (remplissage gel douche, papier toilette et mini bar) n’ont pas été faits tous les jours malgré l’indication sur la porte. Petit point négatif du séjour: dans l’une des chambres pas de rideaux cache lumière sur toute la vitre, donc réveillé par la lumière du jour, et les chambres sont mal insonorisées, et étant donné les animations quasiment tous les soirs et parfois tard, difficile de dormir.

### 604. Jana

**Service 15 von 10 Punkt.**

Service 15 von 10 Punkt.

Плюсы: Wir waren zum 2. mal hier und ich würde auch diesmal gerne wieder mehr Sterne geben! Top Location zum entspannen 🌟
Vorallem das Personal ist wirklich faszinierend sympathisch. Man wird sogar am Strand bedient und das bei All Inclusive.

Spannend ist auf jeden Fall, dass es im April dort ruhiger und etwas weniger Entertainment Programm gab (weniger Musik etc.).
Diesmal (September - vermutlich noch Hochsaison) war mehr los und es war insgesamt etwas lauter aber nie unangenehm und eigentlich auch ziemlich cool, weil man dort echt viel Programm hatte.

Beide Aufenthalte hatten ihre Vorzüge.

Service, Essen und Location war aber beide male Top!

Danke an das gesamte Team für diese schönen Urlaube bei euch :)
Wir kommen wieder 🥰

Минусы: Gute Frage, nächste… (sowas sage ich selten aber hier gibt es nichts)

---

### 605. Samira
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-09-17 · визит: 2024-09-12 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Switzerland

Плюсы: Ghiaccio non sempre disponibile anzi quasi mai. Frutta senza sapore. Cibo al buffet niente di che. Camera comoda ma i carrelli delle pulizie fanno rumore la mattina già alle 7 si sente che passano e se qualcuno parla nel corridoio si sente dalla porta non è insonorizzata (solo rumori esterni non delle altre camere). Personale non invadente e gentile. Mare pulito la mattina sebbene non sia nulla di eccezionale e senza barriera corallina. Giusta distanza tra ombrelloni. Situato su una strada pedonale turistica per fare una passeggiata e a 20 min di taxi dalla marina e dal centro. Se proprio venite a Hurghada è uno dei migliori posti dove alloggiare ma il mare pensavo meglio. Forse le escursioni mare valgono la pena mentre a Sharm hai già il mare stupendo al resort qui a Hurghada no devi uscire in barca. La mattina entro le 9.30 il mare è trasparente dopo no. Internet non funziona benissimo. I lettini in piscina sono sempre presi e la gente mette gli asciugamani per prenotarsi il posto e va via. Impossibile trovare un lettino tra le 10 e le 17 in piscina (in spiaggia invece è ok)

### 609. Atris
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-09-11 · визит: 2024-08-25 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: France

**Exceptionnel**

Exceptionnel

Плюсы: Nous avons séjourné à l’hôtel Jaz Casa Del Mar Beach du 25 août au 3 septembre et avons passé des vacances inoubliables ! L’établissement qui est idéalement situé, est un véritable havre de paix. Il dispose de plusieurs piscines spacieuses et d'une magnifique plage accessible en quelques pas depuis les chambres et de la piscine. Les chambres sont propres, lumineuses et très confortables. Les animations, que ce soit au bord de la piscine, sur la plage en journée et en soirée, sont excellentes, grâce à une équipe souriante, dynamique, et bienveillante !

Les restaurants et bars, situés près de la piscine, offrent un service à volonté, et les serveurs, toujours aux petits soins, nous apportaient boissons et serviettes rafraîchissantes. Le personnel, qu’il s’agisse de l’accueil comme Ahmed, du nettoyage ou de la restauration, est exceptionnel. La disposition d’une petite voiturette est géniale pour se déplacer de l’accueil à la sortie de l’hôtel merci aux voituriers.

Le buffet est délicieux et très varié, proposant des plats de différentes cuisines du monde ainsi que des grillades de grande qualité : poissons, fruits de mer, viandes et poulet, tout y était. Un grand merci à toute l’équipe, notamment au manager de la restauration, Monsieur Ibrahim, qui nous a accueillis avec beaucoup d'attention, ainsi qu’au directeur de la restauration, le directeur chef responsable de la restauration Mohamed Fouad, et à son assistant, le Chef Aymen. Les serveurs ont également été formidables, toujours attentionnés et sympathiques, nous avons pu énormément être en contact avec les serveurs : Mustafa, Cherif, Hamdon, Hany, Mohamed Hussein, et Mohamed Shoaib et quelques autres serveurs dont j’ai oublié leur noms. Je vous conseille cet hôtel en famille entre amis ou en couple ! Si c’était à refaire je choisirai bien évidemment cet établissement à Hurghada !!

---

**Amazing**

Amazing

Плюсы: Our stay at Jaz Casa Del Mar was absolutely perfect, so much so that we decided to stay an extra week after 2 whole weeks! Everything exceeded our expectations, from the warm welcome to the top-notch service. The food was particularly good.
The entertainment team was one of the highlights of our stay. They made each day fun and dynamic with activities for everyone. The evening programs were very well-organized, with a special mention to the mic show and the DJ, who set the perfect vibe everyday. The weekly pool parties and beach parties were also absolutely amazing.
Finally, a big shout-out to the entire hotel staff and animation team, who took such great care of us throughout our stay. Management was always present and attentive, making sure everything ran smoothly and ensuring guests had the best experience possible. I can’t recommend this hotel enough!

### 641. Filippo
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-08-21 · визит: 2024-08-16 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Italy

**Soggiorno davvero ottimo sotto tutti i punti di vista, anche per celiaci**

Soggiorno davvero ottimo sotto tutti i punti di vista, anche per celiaci

Плюсы: Struttura molto molto bella, tutto molto nuovo. Noi ci siamo trovati davvero bene, personale sempre accogliente e disponibile per qualsiasi necessità. Noi avevamo la suite e devo dire ottima, unica nota negativa forse é che nel letto matrimoniale il flusso dell’aria condizionata era mirato proprio sul letto e non é il massimo, anche chiedendo di risolvere non é stato risolto definitivamente. Nota molto positiva sul cibo, il main restaurant, a buffet ovviamente, era davvero ottimo con cucina internazionale. Sono inoltre presenti altri 2/3 ristoranti che bisogna prenotare, noi una sera siamo andati al ristorante italiano e contro le nostre aspettative si é rilevato davvero ottimo (buonissime le linguine con il pesce).
Inoltre mio fratello e mia madre sono celiaci quindi necessitano una dieta particolare (gluten free) e il personale é stato eccezionale, in posti non specializzati neanche in italia sono così attenti. Ogni volta che ci recavamo in cucina era presente il capo degli chef (Head of Chef), Osama Tolba, al quale riserviamo ancora un nostro enorme grazie, che informava personalmente mia madre e mio fratello di tutto quello che potevano mangiare ai buffet, e per qualsiasi altro loro desiderio glielo cucinava personalmente. Tutte le sere preparava dolci diversi esclusivamente per loro. Davvero un’attenzione unica.
Tutto davvero ottimo, molto consigliato.

### 642. Rowan

### 643. Leila
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-08-20 · визит: 2024-08-11 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Austria

Плюсы: Verwöhnung pur! Ein sehr sehr schönes und sauberes Resort, welches absolut keine Wünsche offen lässt! Das Essen ist top, das Meer und alle Pools sowie Liegen sind wunderschön. Dieses Hotel hat für jeden was zu bieten, egal ob Erwachsene, Jugendliche oder Kinder, das Resort hat für jede Altersgruppe einen eigenen Bereich und man wird von allen Mitarbeitern einfach nur verwöhnt. Sowohl unter Tags als auch am Abend wird man von den Entertainern unterhalten, wenn man das möchte. Und für die Ruhe gibt es auch einen eigenen Bereich. Also langweilig war es nie und ich danke allen die meinen Urlaub unvergesslich gemacht haben!

### 649. Michel
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-08-16 · визит: 2024-08-04 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: France

**Très bien mais WIFI à améliorer, sol autour de la piscine dangereux.**

Très bien mais WIFI à améliorer, sol autour de la piscine dangereux.

Плюсы: L'emplacement par rapport à la mer, la nourriture surtout les plats égyptiens étaient très bon , le personnel (Mahmoud Kairi), la propreté
Quasiment tout sauf quelques points

Минусы: -La connexion WIFI est très très mauvaise quelque soit votre endroit dans l’hôtel.Deux fois en panne sur 7 jours et quand ca fonctionne c'est très lent vous ne pouvez pas ouvrir un fichier PDF.
-Le sol très glissant autour de la piscine dès qu'il est un peu mouillé.
-L'unique ascenseur qui dessert notre bloque souvent en panne 3 fois en 7 jours. Si vous avez de problème au genoux c'est un grand problème.
-les gents en BURKINI viennent et demande au barman de mettre leurs CD de musique. vous subissez leurs musique pendant des heures.
-Le petit déjeuné: la nourriture typique méditerranéenne n'était pas constante : un jour il y a des olives le lendemain il n'y a pas , un jour il y a du hummus le lendemain il n' y a pas. en gros vous gouttez et aimez un plat le lendemain il n'y a pas .Dommage. Pour les fruits on aurait bien aimer une bonne pastèque rouge fraiche, bah il n'y a pas et l'excuse c'est pour votre santé ou ce n'est pas la saison alors que dans tout les pays de la méditerrané on consomme énormément à cette période.
-Les shows du soir étaient parfois ennuyeuse.

### 653. Abdalla
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **6.0** · опубликован: 2024-08-14 · визит: 2024-08-09 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Egypt

**Overall, it’s an experience for us that is not too bad, but I’m not going to repeat or recommend for anyone.**

Overall, it’s an experience for us that is not too bad, but I’m not going to repeat or recommend for anyone.

Плюсы: The beach, the open air area.

Минусы: The hotel is nice but not amazing,
Unfortunately we had a bad experience in the check in when I booked a sea view room, but they gave us pool view room (they said it’s a sea view) but you can’t even see any water because of the restaurant and the trees. I complained and they showed me 4 rooms until they gave us a room that is finally sea view (it took one hour and I was already stressed thinking to leave as it’s the first impression about the place, not good check in process), I’m wondering why they did not give me that room first time (it’s a big question)
Also, WIFI is not good at all as all inclusive resort (limited speed, and not available in some areas)
People who serve you are nice, but not very friendly unless you give tips,(first day, the manager of the restaurant, we arrived as a couple and was no table, so I asked him can you find any table for us, he said it’s all complete come back in 10 minutes, at this moment my wife found one!).
The main restaurant is relatively small and is the only place with air conditioner so it’s hard to find seats as people go there and finish food and continue sitting,
The lobby area is small and not enough seats, we had to stand for a while until we could sit down while the check in.
Also the rooms are quite small.
The level of the guests are not very well educated (I’ve heard swearing many times, and was an Algerian guy who was sitting in the restaurant with his friend and told him let me do something fun, and he called the waiter out loud shouting give me orange)
Despite that this hotel is not cheap, but the service and the quality and the level of the stay is not classy enough.
At the end of our stay while the check out, we had a car coming to collect us, but the reception told me that they can’t enter, we will take you with the golf car and put you at the gate and then you meet your car! (Which sense is that!) we have been waiting while the check out again.
Not smooth check in or out.

---

### 675. Hüseyin
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-07-29 · визит: 2024-07-16 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Turkey

**Muhteşem unutulmaz bir tatil oldu**

Muhteşem unutulmaz bir tatil oldu

Плюсы: Ulaşım oldukça kolay havaalanına yakın akşamları yürüyüş yapmak için müsait bir otel.
Temizlik hem odalarda hem havuzda genel anlamda çok iyiydi.
Çeşitli havuzlar olması otele özel koy olması çok iyiydi. Aqua park yoktu havuzlarda bunu bilerek tercih ettik genel anlamda sakin kafa dinlemelik bir otel.
Otelin en güzel yanı tabi ki güler yüzlü çalışanları idi özellikle de restaurantta çalışan garsonların her biri güler yüzlü ve ilgiliydiler.
Resepsiyonda çalışan Muhammed her türlü sorunumuzla en kısa zamanda ilgilenip hep bir çözüm buldu.
Yemek çeşitliliği iyiydi ana yemekler konusunda tekrara düşüyorlardı ama aç kalma ihtimali yoktu damak tadımıza uyan bir çok yemek bulduk.
Su konusu biraz sıkıntılıydı kapalı şişe su otelde yoktu ikram edilen arıtma suydu tadı biraz farklı geliyordu.
Genel anlamda ailecek memnun kaldığımız tercih ettiğimiz için pişman olmadığımız bir otel oldu.

Минусы: Uçak seferlerinden dolayı otele gece 3 gibi giriş yaptık erken check in için bizden 43 dolar talep ettiler ödemeyi yapmamıza rağmen sabah 7 ye kadar çocuklarımızla lobide bekletildik yaşadığımız en büyük olumsuzluk buydu.

---

### 692. Mehmet
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-07-24 · визит: 2024-07-16 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Turkey

**1 haftalik güzel tatil**

1 haftalik güzel tatil

Плюсы: Calisanlarin hepsi guleryuzlu saygılı ve dikkatli. Bütün personel yardım etmek için gözünüze bakiyor. Havuz kenarında bile hizmet sorunsuz ilerledi. Odamız çok güzeldi. İki klima ve iki banyo olması çocuklarımız ve bizim için çok iyi oldu. Odamız sürekli temizlendi, minibar ucretsiz olarak sürekli yenilendi. Yemeklerin arasında damak tadımıza uygun pek çok çeşit bulduk. İçecekler çeşitli ve lezzetliydi.

Минусы: Sabah erken rezervasyon için 43 dolar ödedik. 3 çocuk 4 yetişkinle birlikte lobide 7.00 a kadar bekletildik. Odalarımız boş olmasına rağmen girişteki arkadaş ısrarla yardımcı olmadı. Tek problemimiz bu oldu. Ertesi gün bir de safari turuna gittik. Siz düşünün yorgunluğumuzu.
Odada iki günde 3 kertenkele gördük. Hemen ekipler gelip alıp gittiler fakat bir süre çocuklarımız korktu. Sonrasinda kapi pencereyi sürekli kapalı tuttuk sorun olmadı.

---

### 713. Ahmet
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2024-07-14 · визит: 2024-06-30 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Turkey

**Ödediğiniz fiyata değmeyecek bir otel**

Ödediğiniz fiyata değmeyecek bir otel

Плюсы: Deniz çok temiz. Sessiz ve sakin bir otel. +16 oteli gibi. Sadece kafa dinlemek için gidilebilir. Çocuklu ailelere daha uygun bir otel. Çünkü aşırı gürültü ve hareket yok. Problem olduğunda otel idarecileri yardım ediyor.

Минусы: Wifi bazı yerlerde zayıf. Kumsalda, yan taraftaki Swiss Inn otelininin WiFi sinyali daha güçlü geliyor. Denizi ve kumsalı yanındaki Swiss Inn oteli ile plajı paylaşıyor. Plaj uzunluğu çok kısa. Resif yok. Geceleri çok büyük çaplı eğlenceler yok. Gençseniz ve yoğun eğlence arıyorsanız bu otel size göre değil. Fiyatı, benzer otellere göre %10-20 oranında pahalı. Ama bu fazla fiyata değecek fazla bir hizmeti yok. Odadaki hijyen malzemeleri yetersiz.
Otelin hiçbir yerinde şişelenmiş içme suyu yok. Odalarda, arıtılmış su doldurulmuş ve kapağı mühürlü olmayan su şişeleri var. Ve bu arıtma suyu tuzlu. Çay tuzlu. Küçük çocuğu olan ailelerin marketten şişelenmiş su aldıklarını gördüm.

---

### 727. Sammar
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-07-01 · визит: 2024-06-22 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Germany

**Für mich das beste Hotel in Hurghada.! Tolle Mitarbeiter.**

Für mich das beste Hotel in Hurghada.! Tolle Mitarbeiter.

Плюсы: Gerne! Hier ist der verbesserte Text:

---

Vielen Dank für diesen tollen Aufenthalt. Ich bin äußerst zufrieden – alles war perfekt und einwandfrei. Die Zimmer waren sehr sauber, und das Essen war sehr vielfältig mit täglich neuer Auswahl. Wir wurden ständig gefragt, welches Gericht wir uns wünschen. Ich werde das Hotel jedem empfehlen. Die Mitarbeiter waren sehr zuvorkommend und wohlwollend. Am Strand und am Pool gab es die Möglichkeit zu essen, und wir wurden die ganze Zeit bedient. Besonderer Dank gilt allen Mitarbeitern, insbesondere Sherif und Abo El-Hassan vom Strand sowie den Kellnern Hmdoun und Mahmoud. Danke ebenfalls an Ibrahim Khaled. Danke an euch.! Ich werde dieses Hotel erneut besuchen.

### 736. Natalia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-06-23 · визит: 2024-06-19 · компания: Couple · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: France

**Very expensive hotel not worth the money. Disappointed.**

Very expensive hotel not worth the money. Disappointed.

Плюсы: Clean, very simple and uncluttered hotel. Nice room with reception. Very nice 2 swimming pools on the back side of the hotel. If you are a seaside pool lover. Nice Splash Bar. The food is the same as in all of Egypt. The bed is comfortable. Towels and linen are clean.

Минусы: We really didn’t like the small dirty beach, the sea a dirty puddle. There are motorboats and boats nearby. The sea is covered with a net, but it is covered with algae and mud. Clean sea water just doesn't get through. The food is very average. The restaurant is small. The air conditioner blows only in the bedroom, it is hot in the living room, it is impossible to regulate. Walking through the hotel galleries with strange descents and stairs on slippery tiles was also not pleasant. People with children in strollers suffered. Very expensive hotel not worth the money. Disappointed.

---

### 746. Nigel
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-06-17 · визит: 2024-06-12 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: United Kingdom

**Generally good stay, but there is room for improvement on some details.**

Generally good stay, but there is room for improvement on some details.

Плюсы: Nice decor, the staff very friendly and accommodating, particularly in the main restaurant and surrounding bars. Grounds kept in pristine condition. The introduction by the GM, of the hotel leadership team, was a good touch.

Минусы: I think there could more sugar free drink options. From a soft fizzy drink perspective there is only diet cola. I would suggest other options like Sprite Zero, or something similar, which would also cater for people who have to think about sugar intake.
I didn’t like the fact I paid for late check out, 1hr before check out, house keeping was literally waiting outside our room, to clean which made us feel rushed to leave !

### 752. Tünde
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-06-11 · визит: 2024-06-02 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Hungary

Плюсы: I paid a deluxe room and I got a family room with 2 bedrooms and 2 bathrooms. It was spacios, looks better in real life than in photos and actually I have to say everything looks better and bigger than in the photos.

The staff is amazing, without them it wouldn't work.
I have to mention Mostafa from the main restaurant he was super kind always smiling and helpful. My kid loved him so much!
It was my sons birthday and he got small surprises! The staff played happy birthday for him and even he got a cake as well!

The beach is nice, you can find some corals and fishes there.
When we checked out early around 2am we got 3 lunch boxes for our flight.

We tried the al la carte restaurant the food was amazin!
You can find a french caffee as well and the coffee is pretty good as the alcoholic drinks in the main bar!

All in all this hotel is nice, modern, spacious with pretty good foid and nice staff. The beach and the pool is extra!

Минусы: The alcoholic drinks are not the best in the beach area. At the evening there are some live music etc till 10 pm usully it is noisy if you want to rest it could be a little loud.

### 763. Sara
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-06-02 · визит: 2024-05-27 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: United Arab Emirates

**Unforgettable Relaxation: Jaz Casa Del Mar Beach Hotel Exceeds Expectations**

Unforgettable Relaxation: Jaz Casa Del Mar Beach Hotel Exceeds Expectations

Плюсы: We enjoyed every moment of our stay at Jaz Casa Del Mar Beach Hotel. The friendly staff made our experience truly remarkable. If you’re looking for a relaxing vacation, I highly recommend this hotel. It boasts six different pools, including an infinity pool with a stunning beach view, and direct access to the Red Sea beach.

We visited in late May and were blessed with fabulous weather. The evenings were particularly delightful, with temperatures hovering around a perfect 26-28°C.

The restaurant staff were always helpful and cheerful, especially Abd al Raheem, and the food was delicious. We had a minor concern about the food on the second day, but the head chef, Osama, promptly addressed it, demonstrating the hotel's excellent problem-solving skills and commitment to guest satisfaction. Thank you, Chef Osama!

The hotel is fully equipped with everything you might need. You'll find an ATM with currency exchange, a spa, a salon, and a tourism agency that offers access to the popular tourist destinations in Hurghada.

Finally, our room was comfortable, clean, and well-maintained. The room service team was incredibly helpful and responsive. The balcony, with its superb pool view, was a lovely added touch.

Special thanks to Mr. Khaled Abdel Latif, General Manager, Chef Mohammed Fouad, and Mr. Mohamad Saied for organizing the weekly G.M. Cocktail. It was a pleasure meeting you.

We are looking forward to our next visit to Jaz Casa Del Mar Beach Hotel!

Минусы: Nothing

---

### 764. Heather
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-06-01 · визит: 2024-05-27 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: United Kingdom

**Amazing 40th birthday trip**

Amazing 40th birthday trip

Плюсы: Check-in
We arrived after a 5hour transfer from Luxor. We entered the fresh air conditioned lobby and handed our passport to reception who told us to have a seat. We were given a cool towel and a refreshing drink. Amazing.
Room
Our room had a sea view as booked. Aircon was brilliant, room was clean, bed was comfortable and shower pressure was great.
Food
We booked all inclusive and it was brilliant. The staff are so attentive, coming over to you wherever you were sat and offered you a drink. The buffet style food meant you could just grab what you fancied. Loads of choice for each meal. Great for my vegetarian husband.
Facilities
We played tennis which was easy to book and got the balls and rackets from reception. There were a choice of pools and plenty of sun lounges. Private beach and bars on both. I loved the towel system. You got two towels in a tote bag in your room to use and at the end of the day you hung them outside your room door and they were replaced for fresh ones. We booked massages for both of us for my birthday the next day.
Excursions
We arrived dumped our stuff in the room and went straight to the pool. Within a couple of minutes someone was over asking if we wanted a boat trip, which I absolutely did. We booked a private speedboat for snorkelling. It, was, Amazing!! I saw so many parrot fish, a lion fish, an eel and a stingray right towards the end. Our guide and driver were brilliant.
Extras
The staff at this hotel were fantastic. After we returned to our room after snorkelling they had decorated our bed with flowers and a cake. So unexpected.

Минусы: That we couldn’t stay longer. Amazing hotel.

---

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2024-06-01 · визит: 2024-05-24 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Egypt

Think twice before you stuck

It was very cheap move from them,
We paid like 65000 and they hold us for a bathroom robe
I’m advising you not to pay in advance because if I didn’t I would definitely change them with another respecting one

### 766. Lesia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-05-29 · визит: 2024-05-27 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Ukraine

**Загалом, моє перебування в цьому готелі було незабутнім, і я обов'язково повернуся сюди ще раз. Рекомендую всім, хто**

Загалом, моє перебування в цьому готелі було незабутнім, і я обов'язково повернуся сюди ще раз. Рекомендую всім, хто

Плюсы: Нещодавно я зупинявся в цьому чудовому готелі і залишився в захваті від обслуговування та атмосфери. З самого початку мене тепло зустрів привітний персонал, який завжди був готовий допомогти з будь-якими питаннями та зробити моє перебування комфортним.

Номер був чистий, просторий та затишний, з усіма необхідними зручностями. Я особливо оцінив увагу до деталей - від м'якого постільного комплекту до безкоштовних туалетних приладдя високої якості. Вид з вікна був просто неймовірний, і я насолоджувався кожним ранком.

Ресторан готелю заслуговує окремої похвали. Меню було різноманітним, і кожна страва була справжнім кулінарним шедевром. Сніданки були дуже смачними та поживними, що заряджало мене енергією на цілий день.

Також хочу відзначити чудове розташування готелю - все, що мені було потрібно, знаходилося в декількох хвилинах ходьби.

---

### 772. Noura
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-05-24 · визит: 2024-05-19 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Morocco

**Séjour parfait au Jaz Casa del Mar à Hurghada ! ☀️🌴⛱️**

Séjour parfait au Jaz Casa del Mar à Hurghada ! ☀️🌴⛱️

Плюсы: J’ai passé une semaine merveilleuse au Jaz Casa del Mar à Hurghada et je ne peux que recommander cet hôtel. Dès mon arrivée, j’ai été accueillis chaleureusement par le personnel, toujours souriant et prêt à aider. Les formalités d’enregistrement ont été rapides et efficaces.

La chambre était spacieuse, propre et très bien équipée. Le lit était incroyablement confortable et la vue depuis mon balcon était à couper le souffle. J’ai particulièrement apprécié les petites attentions laissées par le personnel de ménage chaque jour.

Les installations de l’hôtel sont excellentes. La piscine est grande, propre et parfaite pour se détendre sous le soleil égyptien. La plage privée est également magnifique, avec des eaux cristallines et des chaises longues confortables. Les amateurs de plongée et de snorkeling y trouveront leur bonheur.

La restauration est un autre point fort de cet établissement. Les buffets sont variés et de haute qualité, avec des options pour tous les goûts. J’ai également adoré les restaurants à la carte, qui offrent une cuisine raffinée et un service impeccable.

Je tiens à remercier particulièrement Monsieur Mohamed et Monsieur Ibrahim, qui ont été aux petits soins pour moi durant tout mon séjour mais aussi pour mon anniversaire. Leur attention et leur gentillesse ont vraiment fait la différence et ont contribué à rendre mon expérience encore plus agréable.

Les animations et activités proposées par l’hôtel sont diversifiées et bien organisées. Nous avons particulièrement apprécié les soirées à thème et les spectacles de qualité.

En résumé, mon séjour au Jaz Casa del Mar a dépassé toutes mes attentes. L’excellent service, les installations de premier ordre et l’ambiance conviviale en font un lieu de vacances idéal. On en revient rechargé d’ondes positives.
Je reviendrais sans hésiter !

---

### 779. Merve
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-05-14 · визит: 2024-05-09 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Turkey

**Çok güzel bir tatildi. Hurgada tatili düşünenlere kesinlikle tavsiye ederim.**

Çok güzel bir tatildi. Hurgada tatili düşünenlere kesinlikle tavsiye ederim.

Плюсы: Tesis yeni ve çok temizdi. Ortamın temizliğini devamlı kontrol eden bir ekip vardı. Yemekler lezzetli , sıcak olması gerekenler sıcak, soğuk olması gerekenler soğuktu. Ayrıca restaurant olarak seçenekler olması iyiydi .Her bölümün çalışanları hem çok güleryüzlü hem yardımseverdi. Resepsiyon ekibine ayrıca değinmek istedim. Gerçekten her konuda size yardımcı olmaya o kadar gönüllüler ki ve her soruna anında çözüm üretebiliyorlar. Küçük bir sorun iletseniz dahi anında konuyla ilgilenip size geribildirim veriyorlar. Ödediğiniz her kuruşu fazlasıyla hakediyor. Biz özellikle aquaparkı olmayan bir otel istemiştik. O yüzden ortam daha sakindi. Müşteri portföyü de yüksek olduğu için , herkesin birbirine saygılı olduğu hoş bir tatil geçirdik. Tüm tatilimiz boyunca bizimle yakından ilgilenen Manager Mr. Mohammed Saeid ve Mr Mahmoud Moubarek ayrıca teşekkür ederim.

Минусы: Sadece arıtma suyu kullandıkları için suyun tadını beğenmedik.

### 782. Željko
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-05-12 · визит: 2024-04-29 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Serbia

**6 days solo retreat in Hurghada**

6 days solo retreat in Hurghada

Плюсы: The beach is nice and clean. There are wooden platforms you can swim up to. The infinity pool is overlooking the beach and it's next to the pool bar. Cocktails were fine (I'm not an expert), although better in the pool bar than the beach bar. Room was clean, spacious and the bed was comfy. You could just about see the see from the balcony. The minibar is refilled daily with water and soda, I drank the glass bottle water and didn't have any problems. Staff is very pleasant and helpful, both at the reception and the restaurants. The food was good at the buffet, there are fresh fruit juices included, different spices, enough food variety for 7 days, lots of sweets options. The resort is not too big, so it's easy to get around. There are additional pools where you can relax, away from the sometimes noisy beach area. You get 1 free dinner at each of the 3 a la carte restaurants (you have to reserve it online) for each 7 days you book. I stayed for 6 days (booked 7 for early morning flight) and did a mix of relaxing on the beach and side activities outside the resort, and I enjoyed my stay overall.

Минусы: The animation team does random dances with really loud music at the beach. There seem to be a few people dancing with them, but I wonder how many others are just annoyed just trying to relax on the beach. You can get away from it on the other side of the resort where the adult pool is, but then you're not at the beach/infinity pool. The guy in the beach bar could pick up a few tricks from the guy at the pool bar when it comes to cocktails. The a la carte restaurants don't have a lot of choice in the menu and it largely overlaps between the 3 of them. The food was pretty good (except the pizza at Pomodoro which was average), but don't expect anything too fancy.

---

### 790. Shereen
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **1.0** · опубликован: 2024-05-07 · визит: 2024-05-02 · компания: Solo traveller · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Saudi Arabia

Плюсы: The hotel was beautiful

Минусы: Overall, my stay was disappointing.

Upon check-in, the cleanliness of the room wasn't up to par.
Additionally, the bathroom door knob malfunctioned as soon as I entered the room.
Most concerning, the hotel wouldn't allow my friend, who was visiting for lunch, into the room. They insisted she wait in the lobby, forcing me to leave the hotel for the entire four hours until her flight.
This policy regarding guests was not only disrespectful and inconvenient, but it also felt like a waste of money. Since I couldn't have my friend in the room, I had to take her out for lunch, incurring additional costs.

### 791. Madalina
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-05-06 · визит: 2024-04-27 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: United Kingdom

**A wonderful place.**

A wonderful place.

Плюсы: Staying at this hotel was an absolute delight! From the moment we arrived, the staff greeted us with warmth and were incredibly accommodating throughout our stay. The location right on the beach added an extra layer of relaxation and enjoyment to our experience. And let's talk about the food – it was not only delicious but also offered a wide variety to suit every palate. Overall, a fantastic experience that I would highly recommend to anyone looking for a perfect getaway.An added bonus was the spacious and impeccably clean room, providing a comfortable haven after a day of beachside bliss. Everywhere we turned, cleanliness was evident, ensuring a worry-free stay. Truly a top-notch hotel experience that exceeded our expectations in every way.

Минусы: While our stay at the hotel was truly fantastic, one minor downside was the absence of music on the beachside. We couldn't help but envy the neighboring hotel's lively tunes, which added to the overall ambiance. However, this small detail didn't overshadow the otherwise wonderful experience we had at the hotel.

Плюсы: Uns hat sehr gut gefallen dass es eine eher “kleinere” & somit ruhigere Hotelanlage ist und man somit wirklich sehr entspannen kann. Es gibt verschiedene Poolareas was auch mega cool aufgeteilt ist! Die Hotelanlage ist sehr modern & schön eingerichtet, die Restaurants & die Bars sind wundervoll. Das Personal an den Pools/Bars & im Restaurant waren super sympathisch und zuvorkommend.

Минусы: Wir hätten uns ein vielfältigeres Buffet gewünscht da sie Auswahl recht oft sehr ähnlich oder sogar die gleiche war unserer Meinung nach. Ebenfalls waren wir enttäuscht da diese Reise für uns mit einem speziellen Anlass verbunden war welchen ich mit dem Team kommuniziert habe vor und während der anreise, mir war bewusst dass nicht alles immer nach Plan laufen kann aber die Handhabung der Situation hätte ich mir trotzdem anderst gewünscht. Wir haben dann ein 2er Bett neben einer riesen Baustelle welche fast 24/7 betrieben wurde bekommen. (Lärmpegel dementsprechen)

### 833. Maryan
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-04-20 · визит: 2024-04-12 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Kuwait

**Paradise Found: Jaz Casa Del Mar & Its Incredible Staff.**

Paradise Found: Jaz Casa Del Mar & Its Incredible Staff.

Плюсы: From the moment we stepped foot at Jaz Casa Del Mar, we knew we were in for something special. The beautiful scenery was only surpassed by the warmth and genuine hospitality of the entire staff.
The personnel at Jaz Casa Del Mar are the true stars of the show. They go above and beyond to ensure every guest has an unforgettable experience. From the friendly faces greeting us at reception to the attentive wait staff who remembered our preferences, every interaction was delightful.
A huge shoutout to Nancy [ reception], Abdel Rehim [ Palmera restaurant] , Hassan , Samuel and Ayman [ Aqua restaurant ], Hossam [Le coucou ], Mohamed and Ayman [ Beach Bar]. They always had a smile and a kind word, making every moment special.
The animation team kept the energy high with fun poolside activities and engaging evening shows. A big thank you to Body, Sonic and Timor, for their energy creativity and strong character.
The staff at Jaz Casa Del Mar took the time to get to know us as individuals, treating you like you are the only guest in the hotel, not just another room number. This personalized touch made us feel truly valued and welcome.
Special thanks to Mr.Kareem [ F And B assistant manager] , very keen about our special food needs, and everything regarding our stay. Mr, Mohamed Said, for his significant help on our last day easing our check out and our airport transportation.
My experience at Jaz Casa Del Mar was nothing short of extraordinary, thanks in large part to the exceptional personnel who make this hotel a true gem on the coast. Their dedication, professionalism, and unwavering commitment to guest satisfaction set a standard of excellence that is truly commendable. I cannot recommend this hotel highly enough, and I eagerly anticipate my next opportunity to return to this haven of hospitality.
Maryan Mikhail.

Минусы: Nothing.

---

### 844. Scriba
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-04-16 · визит: 2024-03-30 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Switzerland

**Top Hotel. Gerne wieder.**

Top Hotel. Gerne wieder.

Плюсы: Extrem saubere und gepflegte Anlage. Schöne Zimmer. Mitarbeiter kümmern sich mit einem Lächeln um die Gäste und bemühen sich, die Wünsche im Voraus zu erkennen. Tolle Buffets mit sehr breiter Auswahl an kalten und warmen Speisen. Auffällig schön angerichtetes Dessertbuffet. Morgenbuffet mit vielen Brotsorten inkl. Zopf und vielen Brotaufstrichen nebst Käse, Fleisch, Cakes etc. Zum Essen mehrmals dezente Live-Musik. Verschiedene Pools mit / ohne Musik und sehr grosser Sandsteand mit / ohne Musik. Das Management ist täglich vor Ort im laufenden Betrieb anwesend und sucht den Kontakt zu den Gästen. In der ersten Woche leider schwaches Animationsteam am Strand. Danach wurde es komplett ausgewechselt. Das neue Team war dann ausserordentlich aktiv, wie wir es vom letzten Aufenthalt her kannten. Es lief danach sehr viel. Ruhige Lage direkt an der Touristenpromenade mit den vielen kleinen Läden. Preis - Leistung ist sehr gut. In dieser Region eines der besten 5-Stern-Hotels. Ein Beispiel zum Schluss: Wir hatten jeden Tag eine liebevoll aus Handtüchern geformte Figur auf unserem Bett.

Минусы: Animationsteam in der ersten Woche war faktisch inexistent. Wurde aber korrigiert.

---

### 857. Ismail
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-04-12 · визит: 2024-04-01 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Germany

Плюсы: Mitarbeiter sehr freundlich, vor allem zu unseren Kindern. Frische gepresste Säfte. Animationsteam war sehr bemüht. Das Hotel sieht optisch sehr schön aus. Beim Buffet sind die arabischen Gerichte am besten. Zu empfehlen ist ein Aufenthalt in dem Hotelrestaurant Oskar. Badehandtücher werden jeden Tag frisch zur Verfügung gestellt. Wir würden wahrscheinlich wieder dort hinreisen.

Минусы: Die Cocktailrezepte sollten nochmal überarbeitet werden. Im Zimmer kam ab und zu ein Geruch von Abfluss aus dem Bad.
Bei den Ausflugsangebote vom Diving Center, welche nicht direkt zum Hotel gehören, sollte man gut aufpassen. Die Preise sind sogar mit verhandeln überteuert im Vergleich zu der Konkurrenz/Internet. Und die Verkäufer versprechen einem Dinge, die nicht eingehalten werden.

---

### 861. Tatjana
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-04-11 · визит: 2024-04-03 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Switzerland

**In grossen und ganzen: gelungene spontaner Ferien! Dank Aqua restoran haben wir zum genüsslichen Abendessen gekommen. Un**

In grossen und ganzen: gelungene spontaner Ferien! Dank Aqua restoran haben wir zum genüsslichen Abendessen gekommen. Un

Плюсы: Wunderschöne Lage! Hotel ist Übersichtbar, nicht riesig massig und gemütlich. Der Strand ist zwar nicht unendlich, aber es ist eine Bucht))) schöner Garten, sehr hilfsbereiter und höflicher Personal! Sauber. Gute Getränke Auswahl und Qualität für Egypten sehr überraschend!!! Kaffe war sogar toll in der Bar an der Rezeption)))

Минусы: Nur 2 Punkte die zusammen speilen: das Qualität von Essen und darauf folgende Durchfall. Die Auswahl ist gross aber leider Qualität von Produkten ist nicht 5sternen. Alles sehr fad, meisterst Geschmacklos. Selten habe ich was geschmackvolles genossen. Lieber weniger- aber besseren Qualität! Nur in Restaurant a la cart Aqua haben wir gut gegessen!

### 862. Alexandre
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-04-10 · визит: 2024-04-06 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Switzerland

Плюсы: Nous avons passé un excellent séjour dans cet hôtel. Le personnel est très attentionné, particulièrement avec les enfants. La nourriture est bonne et variée.
La piscine principale est magnifique et la température idéale. Les chambres grandes et propres.
Nous avons apprécié la taille raisonnable de l’hôtel et la qualité des différents espaces (restaurants, piscines, réception, plage).

Минусы: Rien de rédhibitoire. En cherchant vraiment, les cocktails pourraient être un peu meilleur et il manque de jeux ou toboggans dans la piscine pour enfants

### 866. Mike
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-04-09 · визит: 2024-03-08 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: United Kingdom

Плюсы: Great friendly staff, comfortable rooms.
Good facilities at the beach
Enjoyed our daily game of Boules.
Food and drinks ok. Sent 10 days so food very repetitive. Barbeque section usually good and very good desserts.
Rose and Red wines palatable. Beer good. Spirits not great.

Минусы: Did not like the loud noises on the beach twice daily from the entertainment crowd. I know they were trying to involve guests but too noisy when enjoying a relaxing tome on the beach.
Organised a snorkel trip with the company on the beach. Very disappointing spent 90 mins on our private boat getting to the sight and only 20 mins snorkelling along with many many other large and small boats around us. I think we saw a couple of fish between us. Hard Coral reef so not the best place to be taken to, don't know if there are better sights from Hurghada. Then we were rushed out of the water to go and sit on Eden Island, which was very pretty but no reef to snorkel over.

---

**Wir haben das Hotel in vollen Zügen genossen, haben 2 hoteleigene Ausflüge gemacht.( einmal Schnorcheln und einmal waren**

Wir haben das Hotel in vollen Zügen genossen, haben 2 hoteleigene Ausflüge gemacht.( einmal Schnorcheln und einmal waren

Плюсы: Die Unterkunft ist sehr modern und gepflegt. Der Strand und das Meer sind wunderschön. Die Restaurants sind alle sehr lecker und die Mitarbeiter sind alle sehr freundlich. Sie bemühen sich alle total und der Service geht extrem schnell. Die Zimmer lassen nichts zu wünschen übrig. Die Animation und Angebote sind abwechslungsreich und spaßig.

Минусы: Das Hotel liegt inmitten von vielen anderen Hotels. Es ist sehr touristisch und man muss wissen, dass die Einheimischen in den Straßen davor Geschäfte machen wollen. Deshalb ist es nicht die perfekte Lage, aber im Hotel- Urlaub zu machen ist sie gut.

### 891. Kamsin
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2024-03-30 · визит: 2024-03-24 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: South Korea

**편안하고 조용한 휴식을 위한 좋은 호텔**

편안하고 조용한 휴식을 위한 좋은 호텔

Плюсы: 도착하자마자 웰컴드링크와 물수건과 함께 편안한 소파에 앉아서 체크인을 제공받았다.
넓은 풀장이 여러개 있고, 바다와 매우 근접해 탁트인 씨뷰가 가능한 인피니티풀이 온수풀이었던 게 좋았다. 풀사이드 바가 두개이고 10시부터 17시까지 샐러드와 샌드위치, 미니햄버거, 파스타, 피자 등을 식당처럼 주문해서 먹을 수 있었다. 서버들은 주문 및 정리를 위해 열심히 선베드 사이를 돌아다닌다.
전체적인 분위기가 조용하고 풀에도 사람이 많지 않아서 편안하고 조용하게 쉴 수 있었다.
메인부페의 음식도 청결히 유지되고 매일 조금씩 바뀌었다. 저녁에 야외바베큐에서 제공되는 특별요리도 맛있었다. 케밥, 꼬치구이, 게, 새우 등이 매일 한두가지씩 돌아가며 제공된다.
동선이 길지 않아 룸에서 풀이나 해변, 식당을 이용하기 매우 편안했다. 뷔페이자 메인동이 부지의 가운데에 위치하고 있는것이 특이했다.

Минусы: 욕실 어메니티가 벽에 부착되어 짜서 쓸 수 있는 튜브형태라 아쉬웠다.
일찍부터 자리를 비치타월이나 소지품으로 맡아두는 사람들이 많아서 인피니티풀 주변의 선베드 이용이 어려웠다.
객실에서 주요 위치로 가는 이정표가 잘 되어있지 않아 혼란스러워웠다.
퇴실할 때 캐리어를 로비까지 옮기려면 엘리베이터를 타야했는데 몇층에서 내리는지, 내려서 어느방향으로 가야하는지 알 수 없었다.

### 895. Loreen
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-03-26 · визит: 2024-03-07 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: United Kingdom

**Our stay at Jaz Casa Mar Beach Hotel was nothing short of amazing.**

Our stay at Jaz Casa Mar Beach Hotel was nothing short of amazing.

Плюсы: We are a family of four that recently had the pleasure of staying at Jaz Casa del Mar Beach and it was truly a fantastic experience. From the moment we arrived, we were greeted with warm and friendly hospitality that set the tone for the entire stay. We were given personalised attention with the entire staff, kudos to the manager Mr Ibrahim Khaled.
The hotel's stunning beachfront location provided breathtaking views of the Red Sea, making it the perfect backdrop for a relaxing and rejuvenating vacation.
The rooms at Jaz Casa Mar Beach Hotel were spacious, clean, and beautifully decorated, offering all the comforts of home and more. The staff went above and beyond to ensure that our every needs were met, providing exceptional service with a genuine smile.
The culinary experience at the hotel was great though repetitive for a 2 week stay, whether dining at the restaurant or enjoying a refreshing drink at the bar the experience was great.
The hotel's amenities were also impressive, with well-maintained pool areas, and a fitness center. The entertainment was good, though would have prefered a variety on the evening music entertainment as it was too repetitive.
Overall, our stay at Jaz Casa Mar Beach Hotel was nothing short of amazing. We would highly recommend this hotel to
anyone looking for a luxurious and memorable vacation experience in Egypt. We can't wait to return for another stay in the near future!

Минусы: We didn't like that there is no distinction between rooms of non smokers and smokers, as this had a negative effect on us non smokers because of passive inhalation.

---

### 897. Dunya
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-03-26 · визит: 2024-02-19 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Norway

**Amazing experience and will def be back.**

Amazing experience and will def be back.

Плюсы: What an amazing experience we had on our honeymoon at Jaz Casa Del Mar. If you guys are going on a honeymoon, this is the perfect place. Let the hotel know before the trip if it something special like honeymoon etc so they will do something special for you guys.

Food:
- Delicious and with good variety. Several restaurants and main dining areas so there is always something to eat.
- Everything from traditional egyption food, to barbeque, salads, dressings, pizza, burgers, soups, seafood.
- Good cocktails and mocktails.
- You are guaranteed to go 3kg up in weight.

Room:
- Nice view and was very clean
- Suprised us with honeymoon cake and decoration :)
- Big wardrobe, safe and a small free of charge refrigerator.

Hotel:
- The hotel is not too big, neither too small so that was a positive experience for us as it was easy to get a table when we wanted to eat, sunbed by the pool and beach and so on.
- Professional and very helpful staff, both in the reception and at the dining areas.
- 2 ATM's for cash

Location:
- Only 10 min taxi / uber from airport and 20 min ride to downtown.
- Staying at a good location in Hurghada is a big advantage when it comes to doing activities in Hurghada like boat trips, safari and so on. I will explain it futher in Activity paragraph.

Activities:
You still need to do some activities to really experience Hurghada.
- DON'T BOOK ANYTHING FROM A SELLER OR ANYTHING ELSE!! They will rip you.
- Best and cheapest way to book activities is GetYourGuide, fixed prices, reviews and so on.
- Because of good location, you will get free taxi from and back to hotel while booking the acitivity. Just confirm it is mentioned in the booking.
- Boat trips, snorkling, paragliding, quadbike safari, camel ride and so on.

Mividia spa:
- Very professional and relaxing spa.
- Don't be afraid to ask for a discount ;)

Shopping:
Shops close to the hotel = EXPENSIVE!
Downtown = Cheap

We will definetly be back! 10/10.

Минусы: Nothing to mention here.

---

### 898. Andreas
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2024-03-26 · визит: 2024-03-21 · компания: Couple · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Austria

**Casa del Ausbaufähig**

Casa del Ausbaufähig

Плюсы: Anlage - sehr gepflegt, wirkt noch sehr neuwertig, leider etwas unübersichtlich! Zimmer(Deluxe)- geräumig, Bad und WC tolle Größe! Mitarbeiter - durch die Bank herzlich und zuvorkommend, man hatte nie das Gefühl wie in anderen Hotels, dass das Trinkgeld die Hauptmotivation wäre. Lage - direkt an einer Einkaufsstraße gelegen(man weiß was das in Ägypten heißt), aber sie lädt auch zu spazieren ein, sofern man die Verkäufer ausblenden kann!

Минусы: Ganz klar die gastronomischen Darbietung, es wirkt alles bemüht, aber es schmeckt halt nicht, egal welches Restaurant wir besuchten, es war alles so lalala…entweder ungewürzt, verbrannt, vergrillt,…
Schade um die teilweisen tollen Produkte - und aber nachdem die Mitarbeiter so zuvorkommend sind, fällt es einen schwer ihnen klarzumachen, das ihre Speisen nicht besonders geschmeckt hatten.
Thema Badetücher, das Konzept sie an die Türe zu hängen um sie zu tauschen, hört sich nett an, aber hat leider nicht immer funktioniert und dann wird’s ärgerlich, mit den nassen Dinger vom Vortag!

UND der völkerübergreifende Nationalsport Pool-Strandliegen belegen, unerträglich, gegen 8.00 sind beim Hauptpool die Liegen belegt, bei den Nebenpools geht es ja noch(in der Nebensaison),- und dann sind die Liegen den ganzen Tag verwaist, weil plötzlich zu windig oder zu heiß?! warum hier keiner was unternimmt, sehr fragwürdig? Liebes Management, entweder ihr tut denn Irrsinn endlich einen Abbruch und sammelt die Handtücher ein oder verlangt Reservierungsgebühren a) das die armen Leute während Ihres Aufenthalt immer den selben Platz bekommen und b) auch mal ausschlafen zu können und den Urlaub zu genießen können!

### 901. Viktoria
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2024-03-25 · визит: 2024-02-17 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Switzerland

**War super bis auf den Start.**

War super bis auf den Start.

Плюсы: Riesenauswahl internationales Buffet, sehr nette Bedienung, schöne Anlage und gute Unterhaltungsmöglichkeiten.

Минусы: Wir hatten Zahlungsschwierigkeiten mit unseren Kreditkarten welches Systembedingt in Ägypten der Auslöser war und konnten deswegen unser Zimmer nicht beziehen. Anstatt das uns ein Zimmer zur Verfügung gestellt wurde, mussten wir auf Bedrängen der Reception mehr wie 7 Std. in der Lobby verbringen, bis wir mit unserer Bank Kontakt aufnehmen konnten und sie uns bestätigt haben, dass es ein regionales Problem ist. Dies passierte alles zwischen 3 Uhr und 10 Uhr morgens. Leider konnten wir uns in der Lobby nicht ausruhen um wieder zur Kräften zu kommen und um einen klaren Gedanken für das weitere Vorgehen zu fassen. Am nächsten Tag funktionierten die Karten wieder einwandfrei. Da hätte man anders reagieren können. Immerhin erhielten wir ein Zimmerupgrade und es wurde immer wieder nachgefragt ob alles i.O. sei.

### 915. Nay
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2024-03-12 · визит: 2024-02-26 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: France

**EXCEPTIONNEL**

EXCEPTIONNEL

Плюсы: TOUT ! Je recommande vivement cet hôtel pour vos séjours à Hurghada !
Position géographique : très bien situé, vous pouvez vous balader aux alentours de l'hôtel sans problème (boutiques, restaurant et bars à proximité) + proche aéroport
Service : j'ai rarement vu un service d'une aussi grande qualité, l'équipe est au petit soin, les serveurs sont très souriants et toujours disponibles, on vient même vous essuyer vos lunettes à la piscine...
Confort : les chambres et l'hôtel en général sont magnifiques et très bien entretenus, pareil pour les piscines. Il y a un coin plutôt calme avec un espace adulte only et à coté 2 grandes piscines où l'ambiance est très paisible et parfaite pour les couples // si non, l'autre coté de l'hôtel est plus animé avec la grande piscine qui surplombe la mer, espace qui est lui parfait pour les familles
Nourriture : la nourriture est EXCELLENTE ! Le buffet est varié avec des produits frais et de qualité et les restaurants à la carte sont juste supers ! On m'a souvent mis en garde sur la nourriture des hôtels en Egypte et bien je peux vous dire que le JAZ casse tous les stéréotypes là-dessus!

C'était un sans faute ! Je vais le recommander autour de moi et si je reviens à Hurghada un jour c'est sans hésiter au Jaz Casa del Mar que je séjournerais !!!!

Merci à tous les membres du Jaz d'avoir fait de notre séjour un si beau souvenir !

---

### 927. Kim
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2024-03-05 · визит: 2024-03-01 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: United States

Плюсы: Beautiful bright rooms. Nice staff in reception and restaurant, the design of the whole hotel is stylish and modern. I like the environmental awareness and the attempt to reduce plastics and waste. Clean beach area. Fast checkin.

Минусы: The staff needs to do a better job refilling toilet paper and clean the restrooms (women’s) in the public areas. During 3 days in 3 different locations there was no toilet paper. I also think the beach entertainment staff was annoying screaming out all activities (volleyball! Stretching! etc.) when one could easily read the entire schedule. Loud music on the beach was also very annoying. Both from the hotel nextdoor and Jaz. 2 loudspeakers on each side of the beach and there you are in the middle trying to relax and read a book. Some people have traveled far and just want to hear the ocean. Not necessary for the spa/massage, diving and sailing and excursion people to walk around the pool constantly and marketing their stuff. The food should also be more varied. Same for lunch and dinner almost. The info is all on the tv but it’s not helpful. It’s so much info and you can’t pause and read in your own pace. Some rooms offered fruit and cookies on arrival but not all, consistency would be nice as it was the same room category we booked in. The rooms are not well sound-isolated so you easily hear people outside talking. WiFi did not work smoothly for either of us (we were 3 people) and it was slow or you had to login over and over. It definitely needs to be fixed.

---

Плюсы: Die Betten waren sehr gut - auch die Bettwäsche, habe selten eine so gute Qualität erlebt. Die Zimmer waren sehr sauber, aber leider auch sehr hellhörig.

Минусы: Die Qualität der Speisen, leider. Für ein 5 Sterne Hotel erwarte ich etwas anderes. Ob nun das Frühstück oder die anderen Mahlzeiten. Es hat einfach nicht geschmeckt und es war auch keine Abwechslung da. Zudem gab es das Frühstück nur bis 10.00 Uhr. Es wurde zwar ein Late Frühstück angeboten, das bestand allerdings dann nur aus Muffins und Croissant´s.
Das Animationsprogramm am Abend war immer das Gleiche, ging eine halbe Stunde und dann wurde Musik gespielt, so dass die Gäste langsam wieder gingen, da auch die Musik nicht gut war. Ich war mit meinen Kindern da und hätte hier mehr erwartet, wie andere Gäste im übrigen auch.

### 977. Evamar
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2024-01-29 · визит: 2024-01-13 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Germany

**Schönes Strandhotel der gehobenen Mittelklasse**

Schönes Strandhotel der gehobenen Mittelklasse

Плюсы: Wir hatten ein ruhiges Hotel für eine erholsame Sonnen-Woche gesucht, das haben wir gefunden :-)

Das Personal war durchweg sehr freundlich und bemüht und schaffte auch bei Problemchen schnell Abhilfe.

Die Fahrt zum Hotel dauert nur ca. 15 Minuten. Ideal, wenn man nur 1 Woche bleibt und keinen langen Transfer möchte. Das Hotel befindet sich in einer hübschen kleinen Bucht. Die Anlage ist schön und auch gepflegt.

Die Zimmer sind in Ordnung, bieten prinzipiell, was man sucht. Mit dem ersten Zimmer hatten wir Pech, da vieles nicht funktionierte (Abfluss Dusche, Balkontür, TV etc.). Nach dem Umzug in ein anderes Zimmer (bessere Lage mit Blick zum Pool) waren diese Mängel behoben.

Gastronomie: Das Angebot beim Frühstücks- und Abendbuffet ist ok. Die Angebote der Spezialitätenrestaurants waren qualitativ nicht wirklich viel besser, aber hübsch angerichtet.

Минусы: Zimmer: Bett verursachte mir leider Rückenschmerzen, obwohl ich es nicht "zu weich" fand. Der Föhn hat ein sehr kurzes, starres Kabel, der das Hairstyling auch für kleinere Menschen schwierig macht.

Wir hatten ein Doppelbett angefragt, was in der Nebensaison bei schwacher Belegung kein Thema sein sollte. Dies sollte bei Ankunft (nach Belegung) entschieden werden. Der Rezeptionist wusste davon nichts, was immer noch kein Problem darstellen sollte, aber eine etwas umständliche Änderung hervorzurufen schien. Könnte man anders lösen.

Gastronomie: Das Frühstückbuffet ist leider bereits um 09.30 soweit "abgegrast", dass Rührei nur noch als vertrockneter Rest vorhanden ist, der Eierkoch ist dann auch nur noch mäßig motiviert. An 3 Abenden hat man uns sehr verwässerten (dünnen) Wein serviert, der nach Reklamation ausgetauscht wurde. Noch besser wäre, den Wein auch flaschenweise anzubieten, wie es auch in anderen AI-Hotels gemacht wird.

Es ist nach internationalem Standard kein 5* Haus, eher gehobene Mittelklasse.

---

### 984. Ekaterina

**Хороший отель**

Хороший отель

Плюсы: Достаточно просторные номера, красивая территория, много лежаков. Отель разделен на тихую часть и веселую, каждый найдет по душе. Персонал в ресторане дружелюбный. Отель находится в самом конце пешеходной улицы, вечером можно прогуляться. Нет комаров на территории и в номерах.

Минусы: Персонал ведет себя избирательно с гостями, есть сотрудники негативно относящиеся к русскоговорящим. При заезде в номер плохо работал слив в номере и даже при обращении для его ремонта, лучше работать не стал. Еда представлена в большом количестве, однако почти не меняется. Tranquil, chill , sun , amazing cocktails , suberb food.

Плюсы: As someone who was seeking a resort that convey TRANQUILITY, I can say Jaz casa del-mar is the optimal resort in hurghada. Very clean , very professional and heartwarming staff.
Chilling , chilling and chilling.
- Food is awesome , a lot of varieties of high quality food.
Different restaurants specialized in many cuisines.
- rooms are spacious, very clean & simple.
- pools are neat all the time (heated pools)
- Mivida Spa was so relaxing and has a variety of services. ( a must try)
- all bars across the resort have amazing alcoholic and non alcoholic beverages.
- views and landscape are breathtaking.
- any inquires or help the staff will be always there for you.
- the all inclusive service in jaz casa made my holiday. ( you will enjoy varieties)
Thanks for a great five nights vacation will recommend the resort to all my friends .

Минусы: A bit more induction about hotel service when checking in.

### 1033. Petra
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-11-22 · визит: 2023-11-10 · компания: Family · номер: Executive Suite Sea View · страна: Slovakia

**Best hotel which we experienced in Hurghada**

Best hotel which we experienced in Hurghada

Плюсы: We were in Hurghada countless times, always trying different hotels but after staying in Jaz we will not try anything else. We found there relaxed atmosphere, yummy and beautifully served cuisine, great facilities and kind stuff. Special thanks goes to Omar who was taking care of our room, very friendly guy doing his job precisely. In restaurant we were served promptly by always smiling Moemen and Mivida spa pampered us with wonderful massages. Equally we enjoyed diving with on-site diving center. Hotel was clean and even though had many guests it did not feel crowded. Room, beach, pools, bars, all inclusive package, everything was just great. We will definitely come back.

Минусы: Small things which do not change overall impression and rating - AC w/o remote control so it cannot be conviniently managed from the bed; after "our" waiter Moemen left for holiday, the service in main restaurant was a bit chaotic, we felt invisible and had to call for attention repetedly. But once they noticed us, service was delivered so not a big deal.

### 1034. Matej
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **5.0** · опубликован: 2023-11-22 · визит: 2023-10-16 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Germany

**Pleasant stay due to nice weather and excellent staff, but likely not a repeat experience.**

Pleasant stay due to nice weather and excellent staff, but likely not a repeat experience.

Плюсы: Our stay was elevated to a high level thanks to the hotel's beachfront, where the sun-kissed chairs and inviting umbrellas awaited us each day, promising relaxation and comfort.

The staff's warmth and professionalism shone brightly, transforming our stay into an unforgettable experience.
Our room cleaner, a paragon of friendliness and respect, went above and beyond to ensure our comfort, especially noteworthy with the delightful birthday surprise that made our special day truly memorable.

The reception and dining staff were the epitome of kindness and efficiency, always ready to assist with genuine smiles and an eagerness to enhance our vacation.

The collective efforts of the staff, their exceptional service, and the serene environment they nurtured culminated in a stay that was nothing short of magnificent.

Минусы: Limousine transfer: A $20, 10-minute average car ride turned sour with a broken seatbelt amidst Hurghada's mayhem, adding to a confusing, unguided airport arrival at night - nobody informed us to keep walking 100m straight after leaving the airport altogether.

Tips: The hotel staff for baggage transfer asked us for a currency exchange within minutes of arrival, seeking to swap their tip coins for a 20-euro note, inappropriate for arrival experience.

Loud evening music from an outdoor event place (until 11PM) disrupted daily peace promised by garden-view room. Lock on balcony doors was broken. Non-heated pools were freezing cold in October (sun is not sufficient), sea was warmer.

The hotel's eco-friendly stance avoids plastic but lacks mineral water, offering only filtered water, can expect stomach issues. Introducing glass bottles could blend sustainability with quality and elegance. The hotel's local cuisine impressed, while European dishes need refinement. Culinary workshops could elevate the overall quality.

Navigating the Mediterranean restaurant was tricky with items on the menu with and without prices. Our quest for clarity was met with a waiter's brisk comment. The evening's ambiance offered live music that was more jarring than joyful. Despite our eagerness to embrace local performers, the off-tune serenade during dinner was a test of endurance rather than an enjoyable performance.

Animator staff: The instructor struggled with clear communication in English. His reaction to the confusion was unprofessional; he aggressively questioned the guests’ understanding of English, despite the issue being his poor pronunciation. The instructor displayed a lack of professionalism and sensitivity.

---

### 1039. Alessia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2023-11-15 · визит: 2023-11-04 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Switzerland

**Ein enorm positiver Aufenthalt!**

Ein enorm positiver Aufenthalt!

Плюсы: Das Jaz Casa del Mar hat unsere Erwartungen übertroffen. Das Hotelpersonal ist ausserordentlich aufmerksam und zuvorkommend. Sie lesen einem die Wünsche buchstäblich von den Lippen ab und bemühen sich enorm, dass es den Gästen an nichts fehlt. Das Essen ist vor allem in den Themen Restaurants sehr lecker. Das Buffet im Hauptrestaurant ist sehr gross, es hat also bestimmt für jeden etwas dabei. Die Wellness- und Beautyanwendungen können wir wärmstens weiterempfehlen. Trotz der Wärme tut es gut, in der wunderschönen Wellnessumgebung für einen sehr tiefen Preis 50 Minuten lang zu entspannen. Die Zimmer sind normal ausgestattet, teilweise etwas hellhörig, was uns aber nicht weiter gestört hat. Das leidige Thema mit dem "Liegen besetzen" ist leider auch im Jaz Casa del Mar der Fall. Diesbezüglich möchten wir uns aber nicht beschweren, es liegt ja an den Gästen. Wir sahen uns daher eher motiviert, früh in den Tag zu starten. Zuletzt möchten wir noch hervorheben, dass das Hotel jeden Tag und jeden Abend für ausreichend Programm sorgt. Sollte einem das Entspannen einmal zu langweilig werden, gibt es jede Stunde die Möglichkeit, sich dem Animationsteam anzuschliessen und sich etwas zu bewegen.

Минусы: -

---

### 1044. Melissa
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2023-11-12 · визит: 2023-10-27 · компания: Group · номер: Family Suite · страна: Germany

**Trotz einiger negativer Punkte, würden wir wieder kommen.**

Trotz einiger negativer Punkte, würden wir wieder kommen.

Плюсы: Das Hotel kann ich empfehlen. Die nahe Lage zum Flughafen ist perfekt. Fluglärm habe ich nicht vernommen. Die Zimmer und Bäder sind renoviert. Toll ist, dass man bei einer Woche Aufenthalt 3 mal in den a la carte Restaurants essen darf. Besonders hervorheben möchte ich hier das Aqua Restaurant. Enttäuschend war leider das Oscar Restaurant, nicht zu empfehlen, schlechter Service.
Poollandschaft und Strand sind toll. Auch das Spa mit den Massagen möchte ich hervorheben, faire Preise. Das Hotel hat auch einen Tourenanbieter, ebenfalls zu fairen Preisen. Der Service war fast immer gut.

Минусы: Was die Lage der Zimmer betrifft, so empfehle ich ausnahmsweise tatsächlich Zimmer ohne Meerblick. Diese liegen weiter hinten und man bekommt die laute Musik, teilweise auch am Tag, vom Strand nicht mit.
Zudem hatten wir leider ein Zimmer direkt über der Lüftung der Küche, Nr. 1174. Dieses Zimmer würde ich auf gar keinen Fall wieder buchen. In diesem Bereich liegen mehrere Zimmer, die alle den Geruch abbekommen, so dass man die Balkontüre leider nicht öffnen kann.
Leider war das Entertainment Programm nicht nach Plan. Programmänderungen wurden nicht bekannt gegeben. Einzelne Punkte sind komplett ausgefallen, obwohl 15 Minuten vorher gesagt wurde, dass es gleich los geht. Auch das Aqua Gym war enttäuschend. Normalerweise findet es im Pool statt. Dort hat man das niedrige Meer vorgezogen, was unter sportlichem Aspekt überhaupt keinen Sinn macht. Es war auch eher ein Tanzen im Wasser. Sehr schade.

---

**Perfect place for a relaxing vacation**

Perfect place for a relaxing vacation

Плюсы: We are a young group of 7 friends from Germany, Italy, France and Egypt. We had a great experience starting with a very welcoming reception. The staff is super friendly and genuinely did everything that makes our stay as relaxing and comfortable as possible. The buffet had variety of delicious food, and great service. There are bars with tasty cocktails allover the resort. The animation team organized several entertainment activities throughout the day. They also organize day trips (scuba diving, snorkeling, desert).
We would definitely love to be here again.

### 1063. Marie-clemence
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-10-21 · визит: 2023-10-11 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: France

Плюсы: Excellent accueil dans cet hôtel . Très bien situé en face de la mer avec une vue juste magnifique, assez calme pas trop bruyant. Nous avons fait la rencontre de personnes incroyables, Abd allah réceptionniste, Hesham, Noha, Islam Gaman, Hani, et Tene au services du restaurant, des personnes très sympathiques et souriants tous aux petits soins, soucieux du bien être des clients, un accueil parfait! La chambre était très agréable et très propre. Quant aux repas : très varié, copieux avec de très bons produits. Nous avons passé un très séjour dans un endroit paradisiaque et nous n'hésiterons pas à le recommander! Continuez ainsi , c'était parfait ! Ne changez rien et bonne continuation à toute l'équipe !

Минусы: Rien à dire de négatif

**Mooi complex met super buffet.**

Mooi complex met super buffet.

Плюсы: Mooie ligging aan een leuke baai met ruime kamers. Het All-in buffet is echt van een heel hoge kwaliteit, zeker de patisserie. Erg schoon.

Минусы: Ze hebben een specialiteiten restaurant voor onder andere Vis. Dit was echt erbarmelijk slecht. Het contrast tussen buffet (wat kwalitatief heel goed is) en het specialiteiten restaurant voor Vis is echt enorm. Daarnaast is er blijkbaar een verdeling op het strand (doormiddel van een touw) aangebracht voor een zuster complex. Dit zorgt voor onnodige verwarring.
