### 1086. Kitti
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-10-09 · визит: 2023-09-19 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Hungary

**Nagyszerű! Szívesen látogatnánk vissza máskor is!**

Nagyszerű! Szívesen látogatnánk vissza máskor is!

Плюсы: Nászutunkat töltöttük ebben a szállodában, csodás 11 éjszakán keresztül. Összességében nagyon elégedettek voltunk a szállodával. 11 éjszakát töltöttünk itt, mégis csak az utolsó éjszaka éreztük azt, hogy most már egy kicsit unjuk a menüt, és igazából jobban belegondolva ekkor sem a menüt untuk, hanem már annyira teleettük magunkat általánosan, hogy már kevésbé voltunk lelkesek a falatozás irányába. Nagyon finomak voltak az ételek, mindennap más vacsora volt, bőséges kínálattal, desszertekkel, friss gyümölcsökkel. Vittünk magunkkal egy liter pálinkát is, fertőtlenítés gyanánt, de igazság se kedvünk nem volt minden étkezés előtt/után fogyasztani, se szükségét nem éreztük. Ettünk-ittunk mindent a kínálatból és semmiféle problémánk nem akadt, illetve más vendégeknek sem, akikkel beszéltünk. Mindennap készítettek be csatos üvegben vizet a szobába, azt is fogyasztottuk mindenféle probléma nélkül. Három al a carte étterem an a szállodában, egy grill, egy olasz és egy mediterrán, ezek mindegyikébe lehet menni egy alkalommal, ami az all inclusive szolgáltatás része, ha az ember legalább egy hetet kell tölt el a szállodában. Az a la carte-s étterem menüi illetve a főéttermi vacsora között ugyan van átfedés, az a la carteban szebben tálalva, tányér szervízzel fogyaszthat az ember, így mindenképpen an hozzáadott értéke. A koktélokat elég erősre készítik alapvetően, de ha az ember kitartóan kéri őket, hogy kevesebb alkoholt használjanak, a végére egész finom italok sülnek ki belőle. A kiszolgálás rendkívüli, a személyzet nagyon kedves és készséges, mindig az volt az érzésünk, mintha mindenki a kedvünket leste volna!

Минусы: Mindennel elégedettek voltunk, egyedül talán az animáció lehetett volna kicsit pörgősebb, bár ez minket nem zavart, de aki igényli ezeket, annak lehet az itteni kínálat nem elég. Egyes napokon az esti program csak élő lounge zene volt, viszont a pénteki beach party például kimondottan jó volt. Aki akarja, azért feltalálja magát napközben is, van boccia, aqua gym, darts, strand röpi, stb. Szóval ezt sem mondanám kimondottan negatívnak, vagy hiányzónak, egyesek viszont lehet pörgősebb animációra vágynak.

---

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-10-08 · визит: 2023-10-03 · компания: Family · номер: Superior Twin Triple · страна: Egypt

**ممتازه وساكررها في اقرب وقت**

ممتازه وساكررها في اقرب وقت

Плюсы: هذه هي الزياره الثانيه للفندق بعد عام ،
‏‎واحد من اروع و افضل المنتجعات المتكامله بكل
‏‎تفاصيلها على اعلى درجه من الخدمات بالمرافق وحسن الضيافه.
‏‎واشيد بشكل خاص بالاستاذ المدير /عمرو سعيد لما وجدناه منه باحترافيه في التعامل مع النزلاء وتلبيه جميع احتياجاتهم ومن الاستقبال والاستاذه / نانسي
‏‎اولا :-
‏‎بالنسبه للمطعم
‏‎فالطعام ممتاز جدا ومتنوع ودائما طازج في كل الوجبات ويلبي احتياجات جميع الجنسيات
وهناك مراقبه جوده على الطعام باستمرار.
واتوجه بالشكر الى فريق المطعم الشيف/ عبد الرحمن
و الاستاذ/احمد سالم و
عبد الرحمن ،محمد و ابراهيم
ثانيا :-
بالنسبه للغرف
الغرف عازله للصوت و نضيفه جدا والاسره مريحه المناشف نظيفه ويتم تغيرها يوميا
ثالثا :-
المرافق
-الشاطئ رملي نظيف جدا ومرتب على الدوام
-المسبح يوجد اربعة مسابح دائما نضيفه وفريق الضيافه والخدمات والمنقذين وفريق الاسعاف على اعلى مستوى .
فريق الانيميشن والترفيه جميل جدا ومهتم بالعاب الشاطئ ويوجد كل يوم فاعليه على المسبح للترفيه على النزلاء نهارا وفاعليات اخرى في المساء
هناك فريق خاص يهتم بنظافه وترتيب النباتات .
هناك منطقه خاصه لالعاب الاطفال بالحديقه ومسبحين للاطفال
في النهايه هوه فندق ومنتجع متكامل وفريق العمل دائما في تطور لتغطيه طلبات النزلاء واقتراحاتهم والعمل على الشكاوى وحلها في اسرع وقت .

Минусы: لا شيء

Минусы: La seule chose qui me parait perfectible, c'est l'animation, c'est presque inexistante, que ce soit l'animation et les activités durant la journée ou bien le soir. Sinon, tout le reste est parfait.

### 1104. Vilma
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-09-30 · визит: 2023-09-19 · компания: Family · номер: Superior Twin Pool View · страна: Brazil

**Wonderful stay in Hurghada**

Wonderful stay in Hurghada

Плюсы: The hotel is wonderful, it far exceeded my expectations. The hotel's "all inclusive" is truly all-inclusive, with late dinner and early breakfast for those who need it. There are also drinks, ice cream, snacks during the day. Entertainment every day until 11 pm. The rooms and common areas are very clean. The food is very good. The service from the staff is excellent. Any request is promptly responded to. The entertainment team is very lively. The shows are very good. In front of the hotel, there is a very beautiful promenade to stroll around, with great stores for shopping (perfumes, papyrus, souvenirs, etc.). The beaches and islands of Hurghada are very beautiful. I surprised myself. I didn't expect to find such a beautiful, calm and safe place. I'm used to going to beach resorts in South America and the Caribbean (eleven in total) and this is certainly one of the best. The hotel's beach is very good. There are plenty of sun loungers to relax in and the water is crystal clear, with small fish that come close to us. There are no waves and it becomes a real "swimming pool", excellent for children and those who don't usually venture into the sea, like me.

Минусы: I liked everything and I will definitely be back.

---

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2023-09-27 · визит: 2023-09-18 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Garden View · страна: Belgium

**FONCEZ ! Poir**

FONCEZ ! Poir

Плюсы: J'ai passé un séjour incroyable à l'hôtel Jaz Casa Del Mar Beach. Le personnel était exceptionnellement serviable +++ et sympathique petit bémol parle très très peu le français, la chambre était impeccablement propre et confortable, et l'emplacement juste en face de la piscine à débordement vue sur mer. De plus, le restaurant de l'hôtel a offert une bonne cuisine, buffet varié pour ceux qui aiment tout manger. (Ne pas oublier un pantalon pour les hommes pour les restaurants dans l’hôtel sauf le buffet). Dans l'ensemble, c'était une expérience mémorable, et je recommande vivement cet hôtel à tous les voyageurs.

Минусы: Avoir un peu plus de personnel qui parlent le français, mais au delà de ça, ils se débrouillaient pour nous comprendre et être compris.

### 1128. Dilara
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-09-18 · визит: 2023-09-12 · компания: Family · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Switzerland

**Çok iyi**

Çok iyi

Плюсы: Tesis çok şık ve konforlu tasarlanmış. Bahçe katı havuz manzaralı oda da kaldım, odalar tertemiz, tesis genel olarak çok temiz, yatak, nobilya, şezlonglar her detay kaliteli ve şık. Kısım kısım ayrılmış havuzlar ve odalar sayesinde, yığılma yok. Sessizlik istiyorsan bir havuza git, buraz müzik bar keyfi istiyorsan diğer tarafa. Ve bu olanaklar çok yayılmadan ,çabuk ulaşılır haldeydi.
Resepsiyon hariç çalışanların hepsi, çok güler yüzlü ve yardımcı olmak için çırpınan insanlar. Yemekler temiz, güzel ama hep aynı şeyler olması biraz sıkabiliyor. Genel anlamda otelin herşeyine bayıldım, çok çok güzel bir tatildi.

Минусы: İstanbuldan malesef ki gece yarısı dışında uçak olanağı yoktu. Uçagımız gece 3 de oldugu icin , oda müsaitliği varsa 12 de değil de 1-2 gibi çıkış yapabilir miyiz diye sorduk. Resepsiyonist arkadaş, aksam 7 de sorun tekrar yer müsaitse tabi ki kalabilirsiniz dedi. Akşam 7 gibi tekrar gittik arkadaşın yanına, bir üst yetkilisine gitti sordu ve o yetkili tenezzül edip yüzümüze bakmayıp yer yok odalar dolu dedirtti. Ben de peki otelde bekleyebilir miyiz hiç degilse bir süre dedik. Yine diğer şefine sordu, ve yine aynı tavirla bu kez oda da 3 e kadar kalmak icin 40 euro
5 e kadar kalmak uçin 80 euro ödersek kalabilecegimizi, bilekliklerimizin alinacagini, yer icersek ödemek zorunda kalacagimizi vs söyledi. Ben otele ilk gün ucaktan kaynaklı gece vardığım için tüm gün birseyden faydalanmadım ve bunu biliyorlar, bu sebeple biraz eznek olmalarini beklerdim. Ve bizim odamıza o gün kimse gelmedi. Yer yok çık diyip,sonradan para verirsen kalabilirsin çok çirkin bir tavır oldu. Tabi ki dolu olabilir, imkan olmaya bilir ama bu sekilde, önce evet, sonra hayır, sonra para verirsen evet diyip yüzumuze bakmayip , diger calisanla cevap verdirmesi çok çirkindi. Böyke güzel bir otelin imajını sarsan bir çalışan.

### 1132. Marian
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-09-17 · визит: 2023-09-12 · компания: Family · номер: Superior Twin Triple · страна: Egypt

**Exceptional stay**

Exceptional stay

Плюсы: I will recommend this place to everyone and my family and I will definitely come again

Everything was more than excellent, starting from our arrival and the wonderful treatment at the reception, especially Mr. Abdullah, who dealt with us with great professionalism and met all our requests.

The room is very clean and cleaned constantly every day

The place smells very, very nice

I advise you to try their fresh mango juice, because it exceeds all expectations

The restaurant needs a separate review because it is truly comprehensive and the taste of all the dishes is very much above excellent and the varieties are very diverse and satisfying for all tastes.

And special thanks to Mr. Haitham in the restaurant, when he learned about my father’s birthday, he arranged a beautiful party for him and had a very wonderfully decorated cake. He was very cooperative with us.

In addition, they decorated the room for my father and it was extremely beautiful

Hisham in the restaurant was also very active and cooperative and fulfilled all our drinks requests as quickly as possible

Everything was beautiful and very, very clean. The rooms and swimming pools smelled very nice. Also, the sea was wonderful. We could see the fish, but it was a little rocky. It is best to take shoes designated for the sea.

All the staff were very friendly and welcoming. It was a wonderful stay and I will definitely come back again.

love you all jaz casa delmar beach staff

Thanks for your help and your special soul in this place

❤️

Минусы: No thing

### 1143. Simone
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **6.0** · опубликован: 2023-09-13 · визит: 2023-09-01 · компания: Group · номер: Family Suite · страна: Switzerland

**Rapport qualité/prix moyen. Les chambres-doubles possèdent bien 2 salles de bain (contrairement à ce que me disait l**

Rapport qualité/prix moyen. Les chambres-doubles possèdent bien 2 salles de bain (contrairement à ce que me disait l

Плюсы: - Le personnel qualifié, réactif et souriant.
- la nourriture est bonne et variée au buffet.
- bons restaurants à la carte.
- la boutique de souvenirs/épicerie

Минусы: - la vue affreuse de notre chambre-double 1224 donnait sur un escalier menant à des toboggans aquatiques d’un hôtel voisin. Aucune vue sur la mer ou la piscine, pire : les gens qui faisaient la queue passaient le temps à nous regarder chiller sur le balcon.
- la clim sentait le renfermé dans la première chambre et vu les températures dehors on a passé le séjour avec cette odeur, pas le choix.
- les 2 salles de bain sentaient les remontées d’égout avant même qu’elles aient été utilisées
- le coffre fort ne fonctionnait pas
- la plage est à proximité immédiate de l’hôtel mais il n’y a rien à voir en snorkeling, contrairement à l’hôtel du même groupe Iberotel Costa Mares à Marsa Alam. En plus, des gros bateaux sont amarrés H24 et gâchent la vue.
- l’attitude du responsable du centre de plongée de l’hôtel qui, pour vendre ses prestations, dénigre sans scrupule tous les autres centres de plongée d’Hurghada.
- les piscines ferment tôt à 18h30 alors que le soleil n’est pas couché, c’est dommage, mais pour le coup il disent que c’est pour tous les hôtels comme ça.
- et dernier point, je n’ai jamais vu de ma vie une équipe d’animateurs nous forcer la main à ce point pour écrire un avis sur l’hôtel sur Google, alors qu’on était tranquille sur les transats. Pas étonnant que tant de monde notent l’hôtel, qui est sincèrement OK, mais dont le rapport qualité/prix était bien en dessous de ce qu’on attendait du Groupe Jaz, surtout à cause de la chambre qu’on a eu.

---

### 1150. Rhea
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2023-09-10 · визит: 2023-09-02 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Germany

Плюсы: The hotel is lovely. Everything is very clean and the facilities are very well maintained. The food, especially in the a la carte restaurants, is really amazing. The family rooms are spacious and very convenient. A perfect relaxing beach holiday!!

We requested to change our room as we got one with a view of a wall of another hotel. The staff were very helpful and gave us a great ocean view room that made a huge difference.

Минусы: The a la carte restaurants require men to follow a dress code that doesn't make much sense. No shorts allowed even though it's an open air poolside restaurant in 40⁰C heat. So men are dressed up in long pants and closed shoes but women can be in shorts and flip flops.

I would have preferred a spa and salon brochure and some info on the kids club placed in the room rather than the staff coming to us again and again at the pool trying to sell some service.

---

### 1160. Dalia
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2023-09-06 · визит: 2023-08-29 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Egypt

**Amazing**

Amazing

Плюсы: Friendly...amazing staff except for the reception bar man he is rude.
Food was amazing...delicious and variable all the restaurant workers from the chefs to the cleaning men were amazing
Very clean with not even an ant around the pool
A big thanks for all the aqua bar men specially kerolos and Julian from the animation team
It has a green star so u wouldn't find any plastic

Минусы: Water wasn’t mineral it was filtered water so we had to buy water
Never saw the G.M passes like other hotels although we requested to see him alot of times for pool issue and soft animation activities
Animation was so sleepy with low music and they said its regards the managment policy as they want it to be calming resort...which was against what i was told by the hotel before my reservation
Almost 80%from guests wanted music...its a vacation on a resort by families not an adults resort
The night shows was almost empty coz we couldn't hear the music coz they had to lower it down coz its location is horrible between the rooms
So it was boring
None ever asked the guests about they stay except once from one of the animation team...but where is the managment.
The hotel had alot of good points that i really never found on big resorts chains but you really need to work on those points

### 1184. Mohamed
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **6.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-31 · визит: 2023-08-27 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Egypt

Плюсы: Location , view, and the staff were friendly .

Минусы: I didn't like the room as my reservation indicated 2 bedrooms with the living room as mentioned while booking, but they checked me in at 2 bedrooms only
The room was on top of the restaurant and barely located to the pool and sea as my booking I communicated it with the reception 'guest relations and the reservation via a message but no action had been taken.
I checked the types of family rooms with the sea view and pool view it had 2 or 3 occupied, and the rest was vacant.
In many hotels, they are making upgrades to the guests, but what I experienced it was a downgrade without any compensation.
The reception team informed us that there are no rooms with living rooms. If so, why is it featured at booking that the rooms with living and sofa come to bed?

Restaurant Buffet is good it had many varieties at breakfast while lunch and dinner are not that equally at the main course variety in some days.

Room Service menu prices could have some review on from the management as the prices based on Euros could make some difference to local Citizens guest's prices also even for minimum of 40 Euros for a main course dish or 23 euros for pasta or Pizza are still expensive for foreigners I would recommend to revise the prices .
Housekeeping team I kept the swimming towels for changing, but it wasn't.

Overall, the resort services are good. However, managers need to look after many things for being the best.

---

Плюсы: Beach was great and food was mostly amazing!

Минусы: The property had a really poor layout! Only one lift and you had to walk a long time just to get to the main dinning room or to the beach. Very easy to get lost in the hotel and do laps due to poor layout. Ordered room service as we arrived really late on the first night and firstly was told what I was ordering wasn’t even on the menu after I picked it off the menu. Then I get a call back saying that they will have to change a few things as it’s not available. Had to wait an hour for some salad, hot chips and Pastries. Check In and check out staff were not very accomodating or seemed to even care to much about our stay.

### 1224. Enjoyurlife4ever
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-15 · визит: 2023-08-04 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: United Kingdom

**From start to finish, the staff, the food, the rooms, the facilities could not have been better.**

From start to finish, the staff, the food, the rooms, the facilities could not have been better.

Плюсы: We spent a magical week at this magical resort with our kids and our friends. From start to finish, the staff, the fellow guests, the food, the rooms, the facilities, the entertainment, could not have been better. This hotel is one of the best hotels I’ve ever stayed in.

The bedroom was so nice the pillows were so comfortable the balcony had a great view.
The food was delicious and the room service food was awesome. The member of staff was very attentive and always happy and their approach to customer relations was exceptional. If you're looking for activity there was always stuff at the main pool to offer aqua gym, dance class, fun games etc. Special mention to the blonde, German guy ((I forgot his name 🤭) and Mercie in the animation team who made every day so much fun.
Special thanks too to Ali Hegazi for the hospitality and kindness in the restaurant, he made our stay memorable. He and his team helped 7 of us sit together and the food choice was always superb. All in all we will definitely be back in the future.
We were very sad to leave and can't wait to go back.

Минусы: Just beware like any other tourist areas. When you are out of hotel they are always trying to get you into their shop to buy something. Area is completely safe though, there is a high volume of police and military to ensure tourist safety.

---

### 1225. El
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **6.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-15 · визит: 2023-08-07 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Belgium

**Average stay, not a 5 star hotel experience.**

Average stay, not a 5 star hotel experience.

Плюсы: Food was great, lots of restaurants available throughout the day so you can always find somewhere to grab something to eat. Main buffet is not huge but has good quality food and the outside tables are great. Pools are nice.

Минусы: Elevator issues, we were blocked a few times in the elevator and there was only 1 elevator in our whole building. The room at first had a bad smell and we had to cover the water evacuation in the bathroom because it was bringing out a bad smell in the room. They were working on the room next to us and even though I explicitly asked they don't paint because the smell was coming through the AC they painted anyways. Most bars and cafes close early and only the lobby bar is open after 11pm. No swimming in the pool after 6:30pm. The beach is really bad.

### 1230. Basma
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-13 · визит: 2023-08-08 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Canada

**I will be coming back for sure. It truly felt like my kids and I were at home here!**

I will be coming back for sure. It truly felt like my kids and I were at home here!

Плюсы: I loved everything! From the soothing decor and meticulously maintained landscaping to the exacting attention to detail by all staff regarding guests every comfort and need. The ultra inclusive experience offers an enormous amount of high quality food and drinks round the clock, the selection very diverse and thoughtful. The pools maintenance was spot on. The lifeguards are attentive, their care extending to picking up your lost items and transferring them to the receptionist’s lost and found. The beach is kept clean and tidy, and the water is beautiful with floating rings spread around the deeper water for guests comfort. The animation team are so talented and friendly and go all the way to make you feel relaxed and engaged with their activities. I especially enjoyed the aqua gym with Julien. There is nightly entertainment going on at the resort, I enjoyed the latin dance show and the belly dance and tannoora show. Every Friday night there’s a beach foam party and the kids loved it! The kids club is amazing and Nadia runs it brilliantly, she is just excellent with kids of all ages and obviously loves her job. The kids loved her kids disco every night even though my kids are usually shy. Ghada at the bar is great with everyone and knows her stuff. Hasanein at the main restaurant is an awesome server.

---

### 1239. Murat
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-11 · визит: 2023-08-03 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Turkey

**Très bien mais ennuyeux à partir de 4ème jours**

Très bien mais ennuyeux à partir de 4ème jours

Плюсы: Hotel très confortable, restauration exemplaire. Je voudrais féliciter les cuisiniers pour leurs performances.

Минусы: Hôtel est très propre en général mais je voudrais exclure les piscines. Les murs intérieurs de la piscine très sales au niveau de l’eau certainement à cause des protections solaires utilisées par les clients mais il ne faut pas oublier pendant la nettoyage. Je voudrais rajouter aussi manque de l’ombre autour de la piscine principale. Les activités proposées par les animateurs sous le soleil sont risqués.

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-08 · визит: 2023-08-05 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Lebanon

Everything there was excellent except for the food which was the complete opposite.

Плюсы: The hotel is very clean which usually doesn't apply to most places in Egypt. The have very good facilities and the service is excellent.

Минусы: The food on the other hand was very bad. We stayed for 3 days and we didn't have any good experience with the food there. We even tried the Italian Restaurant on site which was for extra charge, and the food there was no better than the buffet.

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **6.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-08 · визит: 2023-07-27 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Egypt

**Bad animation team , rest was ok , less than expected value vs. quality**

Bad animation team , rest was ok , less than expected value vs. quality

Плюсы: The room was comfy, yet not a direct sea view . As i booked it , the view from the room is mainly on the pool, and the seaview is partial , although i booked seaview .

Tidiness of the place overall .

Минусы: The animation team approached only once in 7 nights!!! , yet my last visit ( 2 years ago) i was approached daily, and the animation team was nicer and was more professional than the current .. (0/10) for the current animation team

Food was ok , but less than what I expected ( 7/10 for food) .

Overall, to pay around 1.8k usd in hurghada , i could have got a better quality than what i received . This was not my first stay in the hotel , i think the curve is in decline .

I will think twice before booking this hotel again .

### 1258. عبدالرحمن
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **5.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-06 · визит: 2023-08-03 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Sea View · страна: Saudi Arabia

**الإنتباه أثناء الدفع بالفيزا في الفندق وعدم تصديق الاستقبال اذا ابلغك ان المبلغ لم يخصم منك**

الإنتباه أثناء الدفع بالفيزا في الفندق وعدم تصديق الاستقبال اذا ابلغك ان المبلغ لم يخصم منك

Плюсы: البوفيه والاكل متنوع

Минусы: َمغالطة النزيل حيث تم سحب مبلغ الاقامة مرتين وعند الرجوع إليهم لا تجد من يفيدك فورا عن ذلك بل عليك الانتظار حتي قدوم موظفهم المختص بعمليات المحاسبه واذا جاك وقام بالتأكد من السحوبات المدبله لا يتم حل المشكلة مباشره واعادة المبلغ فقط يخبرك انهم سيرفعون ذلك للبنك وعليك الانتظار حتي رجوع المبالغ دون تقدير لوقت النزيل وظروفه مثلا انا جاي يومين او 3 وماشي فكيف يخبروني ان اعادة اموالي تستغرق 15 يوم هل المطلوب ابقي نزيل لديهم 15 يوم مثلا حتي ترجع فلوسي واظل ادفع قيمة الايام كل يوم هل هذا حل منطقي من فندق مصنف خمس نجوم شي غريب هذا الإجراء ولماذا لم يتم ارجاع المبلغ فورا الي خصوصا ان المبلغ مثبت خصمة مرتين لماذا انا لازم انتظر مده 15 يوم مع العلم اني انتظرت لمدة 3ليالي و 4 ايام ولم استرد مبلغي والمبلغ اكثر من 400 دولار اميركي كنت اتمني وجود شخص له صلاحية لحل المشكله فورا اتمني ان تصل رسالتي الي مدير الفندق او مدير شركة jaz المشغله للفنادق وتطويرها وان يتفهموا ان النزيل يبحث عن راحة وقضاء وقت ممتع بعيد عن المشاكل ولكن ان يتسبب الفندق في مشكله للنزيل ويترك النزيل في مشكله ويطلع منها وهو السبب تصرف لا يليق بسمعة فندق مصنف 5 نجوم

### 1260. Claire
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-08-06 · визит: 2023-07-08 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: Luxembourg

**Perfectly relaxing, luxurious, all our needs were catered for.**

Perfectly relaxing, luxurious, all our needs were catered for.

Плюсы: We really liked the sun beds at the hotel. Each one had its own canopy to protect you from the sun. The pool staff were great, very helpful and even offering to clean your sunglasses! The waiters were very attentive taking good care of customers. The cocktails at the bar (and mock tails) were very good. Bar staff were great.
We had massages in the spa which were very relaxing.
The tours were good. My son completed his Padi 1 diving course, arranged by hotel.
Food was good, and again, the waiters were great.

Минусы: I would like greater transparency re pricing. The price on the booking site was shown in euros, but the hotel charges in dollars, which meant that I lost on the exchange, and was charged for foreign currency by my bank. As a result it cost several hundred more euros than advertised.
Some of the entertainment staff were a bit pushy, asking for reviews.

---

The main restaurant and the restaurant at the pool were exceptional. 👌 And I also loved that everything is around you at the same time, like the pool is overlooking the beach, so you don't waste a lot of time walking around the hotel in the heat, which could otherwise drain you.

- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2023-07-26 · визит: 2023-07-19 · компания: Couple · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Triple · страна: Germany

**Não foi o que eu esperava**

Não foi o que eu esperava

Плюсы: O Ressort é muito limpo e a praia privada bem agradável. Realmente conseguimos relaxar nos dias que não tínhamos passeios agendados.

Минусы: Reservamos um total de 4 noites no resort e por esse motivo podíamos jantar somente no bufê, para poder reservar um dos restaurantes a la Carte você precisa ficar hospedado uma semana no local, achei um absurdo! No Booking.com está descrito all inclusive e não tem a informação de que você não pode decidir em qual restaurante você quer jantar. Faltou luz durante a noite dois dias no resort e o calor no quarto era terrível, pois sem luz o ar-condicionado não funciona. No primeiro dia em que chegamos já ficamos sem sabonete pra tomar banho, ligamos na recepção e mesmo assim eles não mandaram alguém repor, só tivemos essa reposição do sabonete no dia seguinte no final da tarde. alguns funcionários não são muito simpáticos.

### 1287. Ashour
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **7.0** · опубликован: 2023-07-25 · визит: 2023-07-21 · компания: Group · номер: Superior, Queen or Twin Bed, Pool View · страна: Egypt

Плюсы: The amenities were very good
The food was great
Everything was clean
The location was great
The beach was great

Минусы: Some of the staff were too nosy, asking personal questions and other things that shouldn't be discussed
- No water dispenser except in the restaurant, so every time I want to drink lots of water I have to go to the bar and ask multiple times while also waiting for the bartender to serve other guests. Also the first day they forget to put a water bottle in my room
- There was a guy in the animation team that kept spraying water on people on the color festival event although being told multiple times to stop
- The colors from the color festival were still on the swimsuit even after washing it and waiting for couple of hours, and when we contacted the staff to help us with it, they only gave it to the laundry but it still didn't remove the colors and now a 200$ swimsuit is ruined and we are the only to blame I guess!
- The check in staff were great and fast, but the check out staff wasn't and I would think a 5 star hotel would have a more professional staff that can also understand English very well, since apparently the guy we were talking to had a little bit of trouble understanding our complaint that we had about something else
- After we checked out and cut our bracelet and just chilling by the pool and waiting a couple of hours until the time of our flight comes, I didn't expect a member of the staff to ask me and my girlfriend that we can't sit on the pool restaurant without the bracelet, although it was completely empty and only 2 tables are being served and the most important thing WE WERE ALREADY EATING. So I didn't understand his request whether he wants to take the food from us or just kick us outside the hotel!
I've been to lost of 5 star hotels and this is definitely the very first one that I didn't feel like I am getting a 5 start service. Not all the staff were rude and unprofessional but there were definitely some good people who were very good to us.

---

### 1299. Peter
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **5.0** · опубликован: 2023-07-20 · визит: 2023-07-14 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Egypt

**They have the potential to be better but they refuse to make proper progress**

They have the potential to be better but they refuse to make proper progress

Плюсы: 1- The hotel is clean
2- The food is excellent
3- The pools are excellent

Минусы: 1- There is no cold water in the rooms as they saying that the hotel is green and provide us with boiled water and when asked the recipient guy replied to cold the water in the room in the mini bar which is so small. We were suffering to get cold water.
2- No cold water in the main bar which is working for 24 hours. The waiter should go another bar to fill three bottles and should leave us waiting the waiter.
3- The internet is weak and not cover all the hotel specially the splash bar.
4- The splash bar is not suitable for the night show as it contains several columns that prevent us to see the show unless should sit at the first place.
5- They turn off the AC in the loopy and all the building as they saying the hotel is green building. So we can’t sit together for a while to play air conditioned place as if they pushing the guests to go to their rooms
6- there is no location in public place with TV to watch away from the road
7- the room is good but the cupboard is so small not suitable for four persons.
8- The bathroom in the room is so big with two basins and two bathtubs but only one basin and one bathtub is having soap so the the other basin and bathtub is useless. However the bathroom is big but the location of the toilet is in a very narrow place.
9- In the last day the sea was so dirty full of cigarettes packed. Cream jar, plastic bottles, plastic bag

### 1303. Hassan
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **9.0** · опубликован: 2023-07-19 · визит: 2023-05-10 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Egypt

Плюсы: It was a nice hotel to stay in in Hurghada.
Booking was an easy process, check in was smooth although it takes a period of time which may be reduced to better serve guests.
We booked three rooms for 8 people. Two rooms were great and staff were excellent to respond to any request. One room had a strange smell and after calling the plumber, it improved but wasn’t okay. We had to complain and meet with the hotel manager to change rooms, and he was helpful and made the process easygoing.
For restaurants, there is a main restaurant where open buffet was provided for three meals (all inclusive) and it was great every time. They offer different types of food during each meal and all are excellent. This is one of the reasons we would go back for. Other restaurants are all inclusive but only if you stay a week or more and they provide a la carte services. The hotel has an excellent sandy beach with a nice seating area where different drinks are provided as part of the all inclusive service. They have many swimming pools, a main one in addition to kids pool and adult pool, with bars around them providing all inclusive drinks and snacks. Spa is available for extra fees. Animations team was great and had lots of morning beach dances and activities in addition to an after dinner show or competition around one of the pools. Around the beach area there is an aqua centre which helps you book water sports activities and even desert safari. Hotel is located on el mamsha elseyahe (Touristic Promenade), where you can walk at night parallel to hotels and you can buy souvenirs. Laundry provides last day offers which was nice to use. Atmosphere was very nice and friendly and everyone was trying to make our stay memorable.

Минусы: It was a nice hotel to stay in in Hurghada.
Booking was an easy process, check in was smooth although it takes a period of time which may be reduced to better serve guests.
We booked three rooms for 8 people. Two rooms were great and staff were excellent to respond to any request. One room had a strange smell and after calling the plumber, it improved but wasn’t okay. We had to complain and meet with the hotel manager to change rooms, and he was helpful and made the process easygoing.
For restaurants, there is a main restaurant where open buffet was provided for three meals (all inclusive) and it was great every time. They offer different types of food during each meal and all are excellent. This is one of the reasons we would go back for. Other restaurants are all inclusive but only if you stay a week or more and they provide a la carte services. The hotel has an excellent sandy beach with a nice seating area where different drinks are provided as part of the all inclusive service. They have many swimming pools, a main one in addition to kids pool and adult pool, with bars around them providing all inclusive drinks and snacks. Spa is available for extra fees. Animations team was great and had lots of morning beach dances and activities in addition to an after dinner show or competition around one of the pools. Around the beach area there is an aqua centre which helps you book water sports activities and even desert safari. Hotel is located on el mamsha elseyahe (Touristic Promenade), where you can walk at night parallel to hotels and you can buy souvenirs. Laundry provides last day offers which was nice to use. Atmosphere was very nice and friendly and everyone was trying to make our stay memorable.

---

### 1309. Hyojeong
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **8.0** · опубликован: 2023-07-17 · визит: 2023-07-12 · компания: Family · номер: Family Suite · страна: South Korea

Плюсы: 가족과 함께 지내는데 있어서 너무나 편안했었습니다. 두 개의 침실과 넓은 공간 및 화장실 컨디션이 좋아서 머무는 동안 불편한 점이 없었습니다. 숙소와 바닷가까지의 거리도 짧아서 리조트 내 수영장과 바닷가를 왔다갔다하면서 잘 놀수 있는 환경이었습니다.

Минусы: 음식이 좀 힘들었었습니다. 개인적으로 중동 음식을 좋아하지 않았던 저에겐 그 향이 들어간 음식을 못먹어서 주로 피자나 빵을 먹었었습니다.

### 1310. Abdelwahab
- источник: [booking.com](https://www.booking.com/hotel/eg/grand-plaza.en-gb.html) · оценка: **10.0** · опубликован: 2023-07-16 · визит: 2023-07-03 · компания: Family · номер: Deluxe Room · страна: Mayotte

Плюсы: Superbe hôtel, les chambres sont grandes et confortables, le service de chambre excellent ( notamment Shérif qui a pris soin de nous constamment avec le sourir). Le restaurant propose un buffet très varié et très bon chaque jour (merci à Hassaneen et ses collègues qui étaient dévoués).
Merci au manager qui nous a mis dans les meilleurs conditions lors de notre sejour.
